/* ============================================================================
   FILE: vite.config.ts

import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [
      react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      // Copies src/assets into dist/ so root-relative URLs like
      // "/src/assets/images/..." (e.g. generate_image output) still resolve
      // in production builds. Do not remove.
      {
        name: 'copy-unbundled-src-assets',
        apply: 'build',
        closeBundle(error?: Error): void {
          if (error) {
            return;
          }
          const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
          const srcAssets = path.resolve(__dirname, 'src/assets');
          const distAssets = path.resolve(distDir, 'src/assets');
          if (fs.existsSync(distDir) && fs.existsSync(srcAssets)) {
            fs.cpSync(srcAssets, distAssets, {recursive: true, force: true});
          }
        },
      },
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: server.ts
   ============================================================================ */

import 'dotenv/config';
import express from 'express';
import path from 'path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { GoogleGenAI, Type } from '@google/genai';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3000);

function getGeminiClient(): GoogleGenAI | null {
  const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (!apiKey || apiKey === 'MY_GEMINI_API_KEY') return null;
  return new GoogleGenAI({
    apiKey,
    httpOptions: {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'aistudio-build',
      },
    },
  });
}

function parseDataUrl(dataUrl?: string): { mimeType: string; data: string } | null {
  if (!dataUrl || typeof dataUrl !== 'string') return null;
  const match = dataUrl.match(/^data:([^;]+);base64,(.+)$/);
  if (!match) return null;
  return { mimeType: match[1], data: match[2] };
}

async function startServer() {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json({ limit: '16mb' }));

  app.get('/api/ai/status', (_req, res) => {
    const client = getGeminiClient();
    res.json({
      available: Boolean(client),
      model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
      message: client
        ? 'Gemini AI assistant ready'
        : 'AI backend unavailable: GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured in Secrets.',
    });
  });

  app.post('/api/ai/assist', async (req, res) => {
    const ai = getGeminiClient();
    if (!ai) {
      res.status(503).json({
        error:
          'AI backend is not configured. Add GEMINI_API_KEY in Settings > Secrets to enable AI-assisted tools.',
      });
      return;
    }

    const { task, payload } = req.body as {
      task: 'inbetween' | 'smart-color' | 'line-art' | 'consistency' | 'timing';
      payload: Record<string, unknown>;
    };

    try {
      if (task === 'inbetween') {
        const startImg = parseDataUrl(payload.startImageDataUrl as string);
        const endImg = parseDataUrl(payload.endImageDataUrl as string);
        const count = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(payload.count || 1)));
        const width = Number(payload.width || 1280);
        const height = Number(payload.height || 720);

        const parts: ({ text: string } | { inlineData: { mimeType: string; data: string } })[] = [];
        if (startImg) parts.push({ inlineData: startImg });
        if (endImg) parts.push({ inlineData: endImg });
        parts.push({
          text: `You are a professional 2D anime keyframe assistant.
Analyze the start frame (image 1) and end frame (image 2) on a ${width}x${height} canvas.
Generate ${count} intermediate breakdown/in-between frame(s) as clean vector stroke paths (coordinates within 0..${width} and 0..${height}).
Each frame should interpolate silhouette, arc of motion, and squash-and-stretch naturally.`,
        });

        const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
          contents: { parts },
          config: {
            responseMimeType: 'application/json',
            responseSchema: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                summary: { type: Type.STRING },
                frames: {
                  type: Type.ARRAY,
                  items: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                      strokes: {
                        type: Type.ARRAY,
                        items: {
                          type: Type.OBJECT,
                          properties: {
                            color: { type: Type.STRING },
                            size: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                            points: {
                              type: Type.ARRAY,
                              items: {
                                type: Type.OBJECT,
                                properties: {
                                  x: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                                  y: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                                },
                              },
                            },
                          },
                        },
                      },
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
            },
          },
        });

        res.json(JSON.parse(response.text || '{}'));
        return;
      }

      if (task === 'smart-color') {
        const img = parseDataUrl(payload.frameImageDataUrl as string);
        const palette = Array.isArray(payload.palette) ? payload.palette : [];
        const parts: ({ text: string } | { inlineData: { mimeType: string; data: string } })[] = [];
        if (img) parts.push({ inlineData: img });
        parts.push({
          text: `Inspect this 2D animation cel frame. Current project palette: ${palette.join(', ')}.
Identify enclosed or uncolored regions, suggest harmonious cel-shading hex colors, and list actionable flat-coloring notes.`,
        });

        const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
          contents: { parts },
          config: {
            responseMimeType: 'application/json',
            responseSchema: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                summary: { type: Type.STRING },
                suggestedColors: {
                  type: Type.ARRAY,
                  items: {
                    type: Type.OBJECT,
                    properties: {
                      region: { type: Type.STRING },
                      hex: { type: Type.STRING },
                      shadowHex: { type: Type.STRING },
                    },
                  },
                },
                uncoloredWarnings: {
                  type: Type.ARRAY,
                  items: { type: Type.STRING },
                },
              },
            },
          },
        });
        res.json(JSON.parse(response.text || '{}'));
        return;
      }

      if (task === 'line-art') {
        const img = parseDataUrl(payload.frameImageDataUrl as string);
        const parts: ({ text: string } | { inlineData: { mimeType: string; data: string } })[] = [];
        if (img) parts.push({ inlineData: img });
        parts.push({
          text: `You are a clean-up animation supervisor. Inspect this drawing for broken lines, inconsistent line weights, or rough sketch clutter, and propose concise clean-up recommendations and recommended stabilization setting (0-10).`,
        });
        const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
          contents: { parts },
          config: {
            responseMimeType: 'application/json',
            responseSchema: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                summary: { type: Type.STRING },
                recommendedStabilization: { type: Type.NUMBER },
                issues: { type: Type.ARRAY, items: { type: Type.STRING } },
              },
            },
          },
        });
        res.json(JSON.parse(response.text || '{}'));
        return;
      }

      if (task === 'consistency') {
        const frameImg = parseDataUrl(payload.frameImageDataUrl as string);
        const refImg = parseDataUrl(payload.referenceImageDataUrl as string);
        const parts: ({ text: string } | { inlineData: { mimeType: string; data: string } })[] = [];
        if (frameImg) parts.push({ inlineData: frameImg });
        if (refImg) parts.push({ inlineData: refImg });
        parts.push({
          text: `Compare the animation frame (image 1) against the character reference sheet (image 2). Flag any off-model proportion, silhouette, costume, or palette differences.`,
        });
        const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
          contents: { parts },
          config: {
            responseMimeType: 'application/json',
            responseSchema: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                summary: { type: Type.STRING },
                differences: { type: Type.ARRAY, items: { type: Type.STRING } },
              },
            },
          },
        });
        res.json(JSON.parse(response.text || '{}'));
        return;
      }

      if (task === 'timing') {
        const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
          contents: `You are a senior 2D animation director. Review this exposure sheet & timeline metadata and suggest concrete timing improvements, holds, duplicate frame cleanup, or easing curves:\n${JSON.stringify(payload)}`,
          config: {
            responseMimeType: 'application/json',
            responseSchema: {
              type: Type.OBJECT,
              properties: {
                summary: { type: Type.STRING },
                recommendations: { type: Type.ARRAY, items: { type: Type.STRING } },
              },
            },
          },
        });
        res.json(JSON.parse(response.text || '{}'));
        return;
      }

      res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unknown AI assistant task' });
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Gemini request failed';
      res.status(500).json({ error: msg });
    }
  });

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    const { createServer: createViteServer } = await import('vite');
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: { middlewareMode: true },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  } else {
    const distPath = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
    app.use(express.static(distPath));
    app.get('*', (_req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(distPath, 'index.html'));
    });
  }

  app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`Koma Studio server running on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer();

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/types.ts
   ============================================================================ */

export type StudioMode = 'beginner' | 'professional' | 'studio';
export type ThemeMode = 'dark' | 'light';

export type ToolId =
  | 'pencil'
  | 'brush'
  | 'pen'
  | 'eraser'
  | 'line'
  | 'rect'
  | 'ellipse'
  | 'lasso'
  | 'transform'
  | 'fill'
  | 'eyedropper'
  | 'text'
  | 'hand'
  | 'zoom';

export type BlendMode =
  | 'normal'
  | 'multiply'
  | 'screen'
  | 'overlay'
  | 'lighter'
  | 'darken'
  | 'lighten';

export type SymmetryMode = 'none' | 'vertical' | 'horizontal' | 'quad' | 'radial';
export type BrushTexture =
  | 'smooth'
  | 'charcoal'
  | 'pencil-hb'
  | 'crayon'
  | 'chalk'
  | 'calligraphy'
  | 'ink-grain'
  | 'fountain'
  | 'dashed'
  | 'dotted'
  | 'watercolor'
  | 'gouache'
  | 'oil-impasto'
  | 'marker'
  | 'highlighter'
  | 'airbrush'
  | 'palette-knife'
  | 'pixel'
  | 'halftone'
  | 'crosshatch'
  | 'fur'
  | 'spray'
  | 'glow'
  | 'sparkle'
  | 'ribbon'
  | 'smudge-soft'
  | 'cloud-cumulus'
  | 'cloud-cirrus'
  | 'cloud-anime-cel'
  | 'cloud-nimbus'
  | 'cloud-mist';
export type EasingType = 'linear' | 'ease-in' | 'ease-out' | 'ease-in-out' | 'bezier';
export type MouthShapeId = 'REST' | 'A' | 'E' | 'I' | 'O' | 'U' | 'M_B_P' | 'F_V' | 'L' | 'TH';

export interface StrokePoint {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  pressure: number;
  t?: number;
}

export interface VectorStroke {
  id: string;
  points: StrokePoint[];
  color: string;
  size: number;
  opacity: number;
  hardness: number;
  texture: BrushTexture;
  tool: 'pencil' | 'brush' | 'pen' | 'eraser' | 'line' | 'rect' | 'ellipse' | 'text';
  text?: string;
  fill?: boolean;
}

export interface BrushPreset {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  tool: 'pencil' | 'brush' | 'pen' | 'eraser';
  size: number;
  opacity: number;
  hardness: number;
  stabilization: number; // 0 to 10
  pressureSize: boolean;
  pressureOpacity: boolean;
  texture: BrushTexture;
}

export interface CelFrame {
  id: string;
  frameIndex: number; // 0-based start frame on timeline
  durationFrames: number; // >= 1 exposure count
  isKey: boolean;
  isHold?: boolean;
  rasterDataUrl?: string; // base64 PNG for raster / fill / imported art
  strokes: VectorStroke[];
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  label?: string;
}

export interface TransformKeyframe {
  id: string;
  frame: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scaleX: number;
  scaleY: number;
  rotation: number; // degrees
  opacity: number; // 0..1
  easing: EasingType;
  bezier?: [number, number, number, number];
}

export interface Layer {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: 'cel' | 'rig' | 'background' | 'vfx';
  visible: boolean;
  locked: boolean;
  clippingMask: boolean;
  blendMode: BlendMode;
  opacity: number; // 0..1
  parallaxDepth: number; // 0.2 (far bg) .. 1.0 (normal) .. 1.5 (fg)
  parentGroupId?: string;
  cels: CelFrame[];
  keyframes: TransformKeyframe[];
}

export interface Bone {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parentId: string | null;
  x: number; // root offset if parentId is null, else relative
  y: number;
  length: number;
  angle: number; // local degrees
  minAngle?: number;
  maxAngle?: number;
  ikEnabled?: boolean;
  ikTargetX?: number;
  ikTargetY?: number;
  boundLayerId?: string;
  color: string;
}

export interface RigPose {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  boneTransforms: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; angle: number }>;
}

export interface ExpressionPreset {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  mouthShape: MouthShapeId;
  eyeState: 'open' | 'blink' | 'half' | 'wide' | 'angry';
  browTilt: number;
}

export interface RigState {
  bones: Bone[];
  poses: RigPose[];
  expressions: ExpressionPreset[];
  activeExpressionId?: string;
}

export interface CameraKeyframe {
  id: string;
  frame: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  zoom: number;
  rotation: number; // degrees
  shake: number; // 0..40 px
  easing: EasingType;
}

export type CompositeEffectType =
  | 'anime-glow'
  | 'light-bloom'
  | 'speed-lines'
  | 'impact-flash'
  | 'gaussian-blur'
  | 'motion-blur'
  | 'drop-shadow'
  | 'vignette'
  | 'color-grade'
  | 'particles';

export interface CompositeEffect {
  id: string;
  type: CompositeEffectType;
  label: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  intensity: number; // 0..1
  radius: number; // 0..60
  color?: string;
  brightness?: number; // -100..100
  contrast?: number; // -100..100
  saturation?: number; // -100..100
}

export interface LipSyncMarker {
  id: string;
  frame: number;
  mouthShape: MouthShapeId;
  label?: string;
}

export interface AudioTrack {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  startFrame: number;
  volume: number; // 0..1
  muted: boolean;
  durationSec: number;
  peaks: number[]; // normalized 0..1 waveform bars
  dataUrl?: string;
  lipSyncMarkers: LipSyncMarker[];
}

export interface ReferenceItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  dataUrl: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  opacity: number;
  visible: boolean;
  category: 'model-sheet' | 'background' | 'turnaround' | 'expression';
}

export interface TimelineMarker {
  id: string;
  frame: number;
  label: string;
  color: string;
}

export interface OnionSkinConfig {
  enabled: boolean;
  prevFrames: number;
  nextFrames: number;
  opacity: number; // 0.05..0.8
  prevColor: string;
  nextColor: string;
}

export interface GuideConfig {
  grid: boolean;
  gridSize: number;
  perspective: boolean;
  safeArea: boolean;
  symmetry: SymmetryMode;
  radialSegments: number;
}

export interface Scene {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  fps: 12 | 24 | 25 | 30;
  totalFrames: number;
  transparentBg: boolean;
  bgColor: string;
  layers: Layer[];
  rig: RigState;
  cameraKeyframes: CameraKeyframe[];
  effects: CompositeEffect[];
  audioTracks: AudioTrack[];
  markers: TimelineMarker[];
  references: ReferenceItem[];
}

export interface StudioProject {
  formatVersion: '1.0';
  id: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  activeSceneId: string;
  scenes: Scene[];
  palette: string[];
  customBrushes: BrushPreset[];
}

export interface ProjectVersion {
  id: string;
  projectId: string;
  label: string;
  savedAt: string;
  snapshot: StudioProject;
}

export interface ShortcutMap {
  [action: string]: string;
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/color.ts
   ============================================================================ */

export interface RgbColor {
  r: number; // 0..255
  g: number; // 0..255
  b: number; // 0..255
}

export interface HsvColor {
  h: number; // 0..360
  s: number; // 0..100
  v: number; // 0..100
}

export interface CmykColor {
  c: number; // 0..100
  m: number; // 0..100
  y: number; // 0..100
  k: number; // 0..100
}

function clamp(n: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n));
}

export function normalizeHex(hex: string): string {
  const clean = hex.trim().replace('#', '');
  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(clean)) {
    return (
      '#' +
      clean
        .split('')
        .map((c) => c + c)
        .join('')
        .toUpperCase()
    );
  }
  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(clean)) {
    return '#' + clean.toUpperCase();
  }
  return '#8B7CFF';
}

export function hexToRgb(hex: string): RgbColor {
  const norm = normalizeHex(hex).slice(1);
  const n = parseInt(norm, 16);
  return {
    r: (n >> 16) & 255,
    g: (n >> 8) & 255,
    b: n & 255,
  };
}

export function rgbToHex({ r, g, b }: RgbColor): string {
  return (
    '#' +
    [r, g, b]
      .map((v) => clamp(Math.round(v), 0, 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
      .join('')
      .toUpperCase()
  );
}

export function rgbToHsv({ r, g, b }: RgbColor): HsvColor {
  const rn = clamp(r, 0, 255) / 255;
  const gn = clamp(g, 0, 255) / 255;
  const bn = clamp(b, 0, 255) / 255;
  const max = Math.max(rn, gn, bn);
  const min = Math.min(rn, gn, bn);
  const d = max - min;

  let h = 0;
  if (d !== 0) {
    if (max === rn) h = ((gn - bn) / d) % 6;
    else if (max === gn) h = (bn - rn) / d + 2;
    else h = (rn - gn) / d + 4;
    h = Math.round(h * 60);
    if (h < 0) h += 360;
  }
  const s = max === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((d / max) * 100);
  const v = Math.round(max * 100);
  return { h, s, v };
}

export function hsvToRgb({ h, s, v }: HsvColor): RgbColor {
  const hue = ((h % 360) + 360) % 360;
  const sat = clamp(s, 0, 100) / 100;
  const val = clamp(v, 0, 100) / 100;
  const f = (n: number, k = (n + hue / 60) % 6) =>
    val - val * sat * Math.max(Math.min(k, 4 - k, 1), 0);
  return {
    r: Math.round(f(5) * 255),
    g: Math.round(f(3) * 255),
    b: Math.round(f(1) * 255),
  };
}

export function hsvToHex(hsv: HsvColor): string {
  return rgbToHex(hsvToRgb(hsv));
}

export function rgbToCmyk({ r, g, b }: RgbColor): CmykColor {
  const rn = clamp(r, 0, 255) / 255;
  const gn = clamp(g, 0, 255) / 255;
  const bn = clamp(b, 0, 255) / 255;
  const k = 1 - Math.max(rn, gn, bn);
  if (k >= 0.9999) {
    return { c: 0, m: 0, y: 0, k: 100 };
  }
  return {
    c: Math.round(((1 - rn - k) / (1 - k)) * 100),
    m: Math.round(((1 - gn - k) / (1 - k)) * 100),
    y: Math.round(((1 - bn - k) / (1 - k)) * 100),
    k: Math.round(k * 100),
  };
}

export function cmykToRgb({ c, m, y, k }: CmykColor): RgbColor {
  const cn = clamp(c, 0, 100) / 100;
  const mn = clamp(m, 0, 100) / 100;
  const yn = clamp(y, 0, 100) / 100;
  const kn = clamp(k, 0, 100) / 100;
  return {
    r: Math.round(255 * (1 - cn) * (1 - kn)),
    g: Math.round(255 * (1 - mn) * (1 - kn)),
    b: Math.round(255 * (1 - yn) * (1 - kn)),
  };
}

export interface ColorHarmonies {
  complementary: string[];
  analogous: string[];
  triadic: string[];
  splitComplementary: string[];
  monochrome: string[];
}

export function generateColorHarmonies(hex: string): ColorHarmonies {
  const hsv = rgbToHsv(hexToRgb(hex));
  const rot = (deg: number) => hsvToHex({ ...hsv, h: (hsv.h + deg + 360) % 360 });
  return {
    complementary: [normalizeHex(hex), rot(180)],
    analogous: [rot(-30), normalizeHex(hex), rot(30)],
    triadic: [normalizeHex(hex), rot(120), rot(240)],
    splitComplementary: [normalizeHex(hex), rot(150), rot(210)],
    monochrome: [
      hsvToHex({ h: hsv.h, s: Math.max(10, hsv.s - 35), v: Math.min(100, hsv.v + 25) }),
      hsvToHex({ h: hsv.h, s: hsv.s, v: Math.min(100, hsv.v + 12) }),
      normalizeHex(hex),
      hsvToHex({ h: hsv.h, s: Math.min(100, hsv.s + 10), v: Math.max(15, hsv.v - 20) }),
      hsvToHex({ h: hsv.h, s: Math.min(100, hsv.s + 15), v: Math.max(8, hsv.v - 45) }),
    ],
  };
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/math.ts
   ============================================================================ */

import type {
  Bone,
  CameraKeyframe,
  EasingType,
  StrokePoint,
  SymmetryMode,
} from './types';

export function clamp(val: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, val));
}

export function lerp(a: number, b: number, t: number): number {
  return a + (b - a) * t;
}

/**
 * Evaluate cubic bezier y(t) for CSS-style control points [x1, y1, x2, y2].
 */
export function evaluateCubicBezier(
  t: number,
  p1x: number,
  p1y: number,
  p2x: number,
  p2y: number
): number {
  const u = clamp(t, 0, 1);
  // Newton-Raphson solve for x(s) = u
  let s = u;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    const x =
      3 * (1 - s) * (1 - s) * s * p1x +
      3 * (1 - s) * s * s * p2x +
      s * s * s;
    const dx =
      3 * (1 - s) * (1 - s) * p1x +
      6 * (1 - s) * s * (p2x - p1x) +
      3 * s * s * (1 - p2x);
    if (Math.abs(dx) < 1e-6) break;
    s = clamp(s - (x - u) / dx, 0, 1);
  }
  return (
    3 * (1 - s) * (1 - s) * s * p1y +
    3 * (1 - s) * s * s * p2y +
    s * s * s
  );
}

export function applyEasing(
  t: number,
  easing: EasingType,
  bezier?: [number, number, number, number]
): number {
  const u = clamp(t, 0, 1);
  switch (easing) {
    case 'linear':
      return u;
    case 'ease-in':
      return u * u * u;
    case 'ease-out':
      return 1 - Math.pow(1 - u, 3);
    case 'ease-in-out':
      return u < 0.5 ? 4 * u * u * u : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * u + 2, 3) / 2;
    case 'bezier': {
      const [x1, y1, x2, y2] = bezier ?? [0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1];
      return evaluateCubicBezier(u, x1, y1, x2, y2);
    }
    default:
      return u;
  }
}

/**
 * Exponential moving-average stroke stabilization.
 * stabilizationLevel: 0 (raw) to 10 (heavy smoothing).
 */
export function stabilizePoints(
  points: StrokePoint[],
  stabilizationLevel: number
): StrokePoint[] {
  if (points.length <= 2 || stabilizationLevel <= 0) return points;
  const alpha = clamp(1 - stabilizationLevel * 0.082, 0.12, 1);
  const result: StrokePoint[] = [{ ...points[0] }];
  for (let i = 1; i < points.length; i++) {
    const prev = result[i - 1];
    const curr = points[i];
    // Preserve endpoint sharpness on final point
    const factor = i === points.length - 1 ? Math.max(alpha, 0.65) : alpha;
    result.push({
      x: lerp(prev.x, curr.x, factor),
      y: lerp(prev.y, curr.y, factor),
      pressure: lerp(prev.pressure, curr.pressure, 0.5),
      t: curr.t,
    });
  }
  return result;
}

/**
 * Generate symmetric mirror points relative to canvas center.
 */
export function getSymmetricPoints(
  point: StrokePoint,
  canvasWidth: number,
  canvasHeight: number,
  mode: SymmetryMode,
  radialSegments = 6
): StrokePoint[] {
  if (mode === 'none') return [point];
  const cx = canvasWidth / 2;
  const cy = canvasHeight / 2;
  const dx = point.x - cx;
  const dy = point.y - cy;

  if (mode === 'vertical') {
    return [point, { ...point, x: cx - dx }];
  }
  if (mode === 'horizontal') {
    return [point, { ...point, y: cy - dy }];
  }
  if (mode === 'quad') {
    return [
      point,
      { ...point, x: cx - dx },
      { ...point, y: cy - dy },
      { ...point, x: cx - dx, y: cy - dy },
    ];
  }
  if (mode === 'radial') {
    const count = clamp(Math.round(radialSegments), 3, 16);
    const out: StrokePoint[] = [];
    const r = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    const baseAngle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const a = baseAngle + (i * Math.PI * 2) / count;
      out.push({
        ...point,
        x: cx + Math.cos(a) * r,
        y: cy + Math.sin(a) * r,
      });
    }
    return out;
  }
  return [point];
}

export interface WorldBoneTransform {
  id: string;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  endX: number;
  endY: number;
  worldAngle: number; // degrees
}

/**
 * Forward Kinematics: resolves world start/end coordinates for all bones in hierarchy.
 */
export function solveForwardKinematics(
  bones: Bone[]
): Record<string, WorldBoneTransform> {
  const byId = new Map<string, Bone>();
  bones.forEach((b) => byId.set(b.id, b));
  const resolved: Record<string, WorldBoneTransform> = {};

  const resolveOne = (bone: Bone, visited = new Set<string>()): WorldBoneTransform => {
    if (resolved[bone.id]) return resolved[bone.id];
    if (visited.has(bone.id)) {
      return {
        id: bone.id,
        startX: bone.x,
        startY: bone.y,
        endX: bone.x + bone.length,
        endY: bone.y,
        worldAngle: bone.angle,
      };
    }
    visited.add(bone.id);

    let startX = bone.x;
    let startY = bone.y;
    let worldAngle = bone.angle;

    if (bone.parentId && byId.has(bone.parentId)) {
      const parent = resolveOne(byId.get(bone.parentId)!, visited);
      startX = parent.endX;
      startY = parent.endY;
      worldAngle = parent.worldAngle + bone.angle;
    }

    const rad = (worldAngle * Math.PI) / 180;
    const endX = startX + Math.cos(rad) * bone.length;
    const endY = startY + Math.sin(rad) * bone.length;

    resolved[bone.id] = {
      id: bone.id,
      startX,
      startY,
      endX,
      endY,
      worldAngle,
    };
    return resolved[bone.id];
  };

  for (const b of bones) {
    resolveOne(b);
  }
  return resolved;
}

/**
 * 2-bone analytic Inverse Kinematics solver (law of cosines).
 * Returns updated local angles (in degrees) for upper and lower bones to reach (targetX, targetY).
 */
export function solveTwoBoneIK(
  rootX: number,
  rootY: number,
  parentWorldAngle: number,
  len1: number,
  len2: number,
  targetX: number,
  targetY: number,
  bendDirection: 1 | -1 = 1
): { angle1Local: number; angle2Local: number } {
  const dx = targetX - rootX;
  const dy = targetY - rootY;
  const dist = clamp(Math.hypot(dx, dy), Math.abs(len1 - len2) + 0.001, len1 + len2 - 0.001);

  const targetAngle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
  const cosA1 = clamp(
    (len1 * len1 + dist * dist - len2 * len2) / (2 * len1 * dist),
    -1,
    1
  );
  const cosA2 = clamp(
    (len1 * len1 + len2 * len2 - dist * dist) / (2 * len1 * len2),
    -1,
    1
  );

  const a1 = Math.acos(cosA1);
  const worldAngle1 = targetAngle - bendDirection * a1;
  const interiorAngle2 = Math.acos(cosA2);
  const localAngle2Rad = bendDirection * (Math.PI - interiorAngle2);

  const worldDeg1 = (worldAngle1 * 180) / Math.PI;
  const localDeg1 = worldDeg1 - parentWorldAngle;
  const localDeg2 = (localAngle2Rad * 180) / Math.PI;

  return {
    angle1Local: localDeg1,
    angle2Local: localDeg2,
  };
}

export interface ResolvedCamera {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  zoom: number;
  rotation: number;
  shakeOffsetX: number;
  shakeOffsetY: number;
}

export function resolveCameraAtFrame(
  keyframes: CameraKeyframe[],
  frame: number
): ResolvedCamera {
  if (keyframes.length === 0) {
    return { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1, rotation: 0, shakeOffsetX: 0, shakeOffsetY: 0 };
  }
  const sorted = [...keyframes].sort((a, b) => a.frame - b.frame);
  if (frame <= sorted[0].frame) {
    const k = sorted[0];
    const [sx, sy] = deterministicShake(frame, k.shake);
    return { x: k.x, y: k.y, zoom: k.zoom, rotation: k.rotation, shakeOffsetX: sx, shakeOffsetY: sy };
  }
  if (frame >= sorted[sorted.length - 1].frame) {
    const k = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
    const [sx, sy] = deterministicShake(frame, k.shake);
    return { x: k.x, y: k.y, zoom: k.zoom, rotation: k.rotation, shakeOffsetX: sx, shakeOffsetY: sy };
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = sorted[i];
    const b = sorted[i + 1];
    if (frame >= a.frame && frame <= b.frame) {
      const span = Math.max(1, b.frame - a.frame);
      const rawT = (frame - a.frame) / span;
      const t = applyEasing(rawT, a.easing);
      const shake = lerp(a.shake, b.shake, t);
      const [sx, sy] = deterministicShake(frame, shake);
      return {
        x: lerp(a.x, b.x, t),
        y: lerp(a.y, b.y, t),
        zoom: lerp(a.zoom, b.zoom, t),
        rotation: lerp(a.rotation, b.rotation, t),
        shakeOffsetX: sx,
        shakeOffsetY: sy,
      };
    }
  }
  return { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1, rotation: 0, shakeOffsetX: 0, shakeOffsetY: 0 };
}

function deterministicShake(frame: number, amplitude: number): [number, number] {
  if (amplitude <= 0.01) return [0, 0];
  const sx = Math.sin(frame * 12.9898 + 1.23) * amplitude;
  const sy = Math.cos(frame * 78.233 + 4.56) * amplitude;
  return [sx, sy];
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/timeline.ts
   ============================================================================ */

import { applyEasing, lerp } from './math';
import type { CelFrame, Layer, TransformKeyframe } from './types';

export interface ResolvedLayerTransform {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  scaleX: number;
  scaleY: number;
  rotation: number;
  opacity: number;
}

/**
 * Resolves the active cel drawing on a layer at a given timeline frame.
 * Supports exposure duration holds (e.g. drawing on 2s, 3s, holds).
 */
export function resolveCelAtFrame(layer: Layer, frame: number): CelFrame | null {
  if (layer.cels.length === 0) return null;
  const sorted = [...layer.cels].sort((a, b) => a.frameIndex - b.frameIndex);

  for (let i = sorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const cel = sorted[i];
    const end = cel.frameIndex + Math.max(1, cel.durationFrames) - 1;
    if (frame >= cel.frameIndex && frame <= end) {
      return cel;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Returns previous and next unique cel frames for Onion Skinning.
 */
export function getOnionSkinNeighbors(
  layer: Layer,
  currentFrame: number,
  prevCount: number,
  nextCount: number
): { prev: { cel: CelFrame; distance: number }[]; next: { cel: CelFrame; distance: number }[] } {
  const sorted = [...layer.cels].sort((a, b) => a.frameIndex - b.frameIndex);
  const current = resolveCelAtFrame(layer, currentFrame);

  const prevList: { cel: CelFrame; distance: number }[] = [];
  const nextList: { cel: CelFrame; distance: number }[] = [];

  for (const cel of sorted) {
    if (current && cel.id === current.id) continue;
    if (cel.frameIndex < currentFrame) {
      prevList.push({ cel, distance: 0 });
    } else if (cel.frameIndex > currentFrame) {
      nextList.push({ cel, distance: 0 });
    }
  }

  const prev = prevList
    .slice(-Math.max(0, prevCount))
    .reverse()
    .map((item, idx) => ({ cel: item.cel, distance: idx + 1 }));

  const next = nextList
    .slice(0, Math.max(0, nextCount))
    .map((item, idx) => ({ cel: item.cel, distance: idx + 1 }));

  return { prev, next };
}

/**
 * Interpolates layer transform & opacity keyframes at a timeline frame.
 */
export function resolveLayerTransformAtFrame(
  keyframes: TransformKeyframe[],
  frame: number
): ResolvedLayerTransform {
  if (keyframes.length === 0) {
    return { x: 0, y: 0, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, rotation: 0, opacity: 1 };
  }
  const sorted = [...keyframes].sort((a, b) => a.frame - b.frame);
  if (frame <= sorted[0].frame) {
    const k = sorted[0];
    return {
      x: k.x,
      y: k.y,
      scaleX: k.scaleX,
      scaleY: k.scaleY,
      rotation: k.rotation,
      opacity: k.opacity,
    };
  }
  if (frame >= sorted[sorted.length - 1].frame) {
    const k = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
    return {
      x: k.x,
      y: k.y,
      scaleX: k.scaleX,
      scaleY: k.scaleY,
      rotation: k.rotation,
      opacity: k.opacity,
    };
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length - 1; i++) {
    const a = sorted[i];
    const b = sorted[i + 1];
    if (frame >= a.frame && frame <= b.frame) {
      const span = Math.max(1, b.frame - a.frame);
      const rawT = (frame - a.frame) / span;
      const t = applyEasing(rawT, a.easing, a.bezier);
      return {
        x: lerp(a.x, b.x, t),
        y: lerp(a.y, b.y, t),
        scaleX: lerp(a.scaleX, b.scaleX, t),
        scaleY: lerp(a.scaleY, b.scaleY, t),
        rotation: lerp(a.rotation, b.rotation, t),
        opacity: lerp(a.opacity, b.opacity, t),
      };
    }
  }
  return { x: 0, y: 0, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, rotation: 0, opacity: 1 };
}

export function insertOrUpdateCel(
  cels: CelFrame[],
  newCel: CelFrame
): CelFrame[] {
  const filtered = cels.filter((c) => c.frameIndex !== newCel.frameIndex);
  return [...filtered, newCel].sort((a, b) => a.frameIndex - b.frameIndex);
}

export function moveCelToFrame(
  cels: CelFrame[],
  celId: string,
  targetFrameIndex: number
): CelFrame[] {
  const target = Math.max(0, targetFrameIndex);
  const moving = cels.find((c) => c.id === celId);
  if (!moving) return cels;

  const others = cels.filter((c) => c.id !== celId && c.frameIndex !== target);
  return [...others, { ...moving, frameIndex: target }].sort(
    (a, b) => a.frameIndex - b.frameIndex
  );
}

export function duplicateCelAfter(
  cels: CelFrame[],
  celId: string,
  newId: string
): CelFrame[] {
  const source = cels.find((c) => c.id === celId);
  if (!source) return cels;
  const nextIndex = source.frameIndex + Math.max(1, source.durationFrames);
  // Shift subsequent cels right if colliding
  const shifted = cels.map((c) =>
    c.frameIndex >= nextIndex
      ? { ...c, frameIndex: c.frameIndex + Math.max(1, source.durationFrames) }
      : c
  );
  const copy: CelFrame = {
    ...source,
    id: newId,
    frameIndex: nextIndex,
    strokes: source.strokes.map((s) => ({
      ...s,
      id: `${s.id}-copy-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      points: s.points.map((p) => ({ ...p })),
    })),
  };
  return [...shifted, copy].sort((a, b) => a.frameIndex - b.frameIndex);
}

export function deleteCelById(cels: CelFrame[], celId: string): CelFrame[] {
  if (cels.length <= 1) return cels;
  return cels.filter((c) => c.id !== celId);
}

import type {
  BrushPreset,
  CompositeEffect,
  RigState,
  Scene,
  ShortcutMap,
  StudioProject,
} from './types';

export const DEFAULT_PALETTE: string[] = [
  '#F3F4F6',
  '#17191D',
  '#8B7CFF',
  '#64D6C5',
  '#FF6B6B',
  '#FFD166',
  '#4EA8DE',
  '#FF8FAB',

  '#3D405B',
  '#E07A5F',
  '#81B29A',

export const BRUSH_TEXTURE_CATALOG: {

function makeArcPoints(
  cx: number,
  cy: number,
  rx: number,
  ry: number,
  steps = 22
) {
  const pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const a = (i / steps) * Math.PI * 2;
    pts.push({
      x: cx + Math.cos(a) * rx,
      y: cy + Math.sin(a) * ry,
      pressure: 0.7 + 0.25 * Math.sin(a * 2),
    });
  }
  return pts;
}

export function createDemoScene(id = 'scene-1', name = 'Cut 01 — Meteor Bounce'): Scene {
  const width = 1280;
  const height = 720;

  return {
    id,
    name,
    width,
    height,
    fps: 12,
    totalFrames: 24,
    transparentBg: false,
    bgColor: '#1B1E26',
    layers: [
      {
        id: 'layer-bg',
        name: 'BG Skyline',
        type: 'background',
        visible: true,
        locked: false,
        clippingMask: false,
        blendMode: 'normal',
        opacity: 1,
        parallaxDepth: 0.4,
        cels: [
          {
            id: 'cel-bg-0',
            frameIndex: 0,
            durationFrames: 24,
            isKey: true,
            strokes: [
              {
                id: 'bg-horizon',
                tool: 'line',
                color: '#3D4457',
                size: 3,
                opacity: 0.9,
                hardness: 0.9,
                texture: 'smooth',
                points: [
                  { x: 60, y: 550, pressure: 1 },
                  { x: 1220, y: 550, pressure: 1 },
                ],
              },

export function createBlankScene(
  id: string,
  name: string,
  width = 1920,
  height = 1080,
  fps: 12 | 24 | 25 | 30 = 24
): Scene {

    id,

    width,
    height,
    fps,
    totalFrames: 48,
    transparentBg: false,
    bgColor: '#1E2128',
    layers: [
      {
        id: `layer-rough-${Date.now()}`,
        name: 'Rough Animation',
        type: 'cel',
        visible: true,
        locked: false,
        clippingMask: false,
        blendMode: 'normal',
        opacity: 1,
        parallaxDepth: 1,
        cels: [
          {
            id: `cel-init-${Date.now()}`,
            frameIndex: 0,
            durationFrames: 2,
            isKey: true,
            strokes: [],
          },
        ],
        keyframes: [],
      },
    ],
    rig: createDefaultRig(width, height),
    cameraKeyframes: [
      { id: 'cam-0', frame: 0, x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1, rotation: 0, shake: 0, easing: 'linear' },
    ],
    effects: createDefaultEffects().map((fx) => ({ ...fx, enabled: false })),
    audioTracks: [],
    markers: [],
    references: [],
  };
}

export function createInitialProject(): StudioProject {
  const scene = createDemoScene();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    formatVersion: '1.0',
    id: 'proj-koma-demo',
    name: 'Meteor Dash — Episode 01',
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
    activeSceneId: scene.id,
    scenes: [scene],
    palette: DEFAULT_PALETTE,
    customBrushes: DEFAULT_BRUSH_PRESETS,
  };
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/projectStore.ts
   ============================================================================ */

import type { ProjectVersion, StudioProject } from './types';

const DB_NAME = 'koma-animation-studio-db';
const DB_VERSION = 1;
const STORE_AUTOSAVE = 'autosave';
const STORE_VERSIONS = 'versions';
const AUTOSAVE_KEY = 'latest-project';

function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (typeof indexedDB === 'undefined') {
      reject(new Error('IndexedDB unavailable'));
      return;
    }
    const req = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
    req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const db = req.result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_AUTOSAVE)) {
        db.createObjectStore(STORE_AUTOSAVE);
      }
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_VERSIONS)) {
        db.createObjectStore(STORE_VERSIONS, { keyPath: 'id' });
      }
    };
    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
    req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
  });
}

export async function saveAutosaveProject(project: StudioProject): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const db = await openDb();
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = db.transaction(STORE_AUTOSAVE, 'readwrite');
      const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_AUTOSAVE);
      store.put(
        {
          project,
          savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        },
        AUTOSAVE_KEY
      );
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
    });
  } catch {
    // Fallback to localStorage for environments with restricted IndexedDB
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('koma_autosave_fallback', JSON.stringify(project));
    } catch {
      // Ignore quota exceeded
    }
  }
}

export async function loadAutosaveProject(): Promise<{
  project: StudioProject;
  savedAt: string;
} | null> {
  try {
    const db = await openDb();
    const res = await new Promise<{ project: StudioProject; savedAt: string } | undefined>(
      (resolve, reject) => {
        const tx = db.transaction(STORE_AUTOSAVE, 'readonly');
        const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_AUTOSAVE);
        const req = store.get(AUTOSAVE_KEY);
        req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
        req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
      }
    );
    if (res && res.project && res.project.formatVersion === '1.0') {
      return res;
    }
  } catch {
    // Fallback check
  }

  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem('koma_autosave_fallback');
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as StudioProject;
      if (parsed && parsed.formatVersion === '1.0') {
        return { project: parsed, savedAt: parsed.updatedAt };
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore invalid JSON
  }
  return null;
}

export async function saveVersionSnapshot(
  project: StudioProject,
  label: string
): Promise<ProjectVersion> {
  const version: ProjectVersion = {
    id: `ver-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
    projectId: project.id,
    label,
    savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    snapshot: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(project)) as StudioProject,
  };
  try {
    const db = await openDb();
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = db.transaction(STORE_VERSIONS, 'readwrite');
      tx.objectStore(STORE_VERSIONS).put(version);
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
    });
  } catch {
    // Best effort
  }
  return version;
}

export async function listProjectVersions(): Promise<ProjectVersion[]> {
  try {
    const db = await openDb();
    return await new Promise<ProjectVersion[]>((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = db.transaction(STORE_VERSIONS, 'readonly');
      const req = tx.objectStore(STORE_VERSIONS).getAll();
      req.onsuccess = () => {
        const items = (req.result as ProjectVersion[]) ?? [];
        items.sort((a, b) => b.savedAt.localeCompare(a.savedAt));
        resolve(items.slice(0, 20));
      };
      req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    });
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function serializeProjectFile(project: StudioProject): string {
  return JSON.stringify(project, null, 2);
}

export function parseProjectFile(jsonText: string): StudioProject {
  const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonText) as StudioProject;
  if (
    !parsed ||
    parsed.formatVersion !== '1.0' ||
    !Array.isArray(parsed.scenes) ||
    parsed.scenes.length === 0
  ) {
    throw new Error('Invalid Koma Studio project file (.koma.json)');
  }
  return parsed;
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/gifEncoder.ts
   ============================================================================ */

/**
 * Pure TypeScript GIF89a animated GIF encoder (6-8-6 uniform RGB palette + LZW compression).
 * Runs completely client-side with zero external workers or dependencies.
 */

export interface GifFrameInput {
  rgba: Uint8ClampedArray;
  delayCs: number; // centiseconds (1/100th sec)
}

function buildUniformPalette(): { palette: Uint8Array; mapRgb: (r: number, g: number, b: number) => number } {
  // 6x8x5 = 240 color cube + 16 grayscale ramp = 256 colors
  const palette = new Uint8Array(256 * 3);
  let idx = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) {
    for (let g = 0; g < 8; g++) {
      for (let b = 0; b < 5; b++) {
        palette[idx * 3] = Math.round((r * 255) / 5);
        palette[idx * 3 + 1] = Math.round((g * 255) / 7);
        palette[idx * 3 + 2] = Math.round((b * 255) / 4);
        idx++;
      }
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
    const v = Math.round((i * 255) / 15);
    palette[idx * 3] = v;
    palette[idx * 3 + 1] = v;
    palette[idx * 3 + 2] = v;
    idx++;
  }

  const mapRgb = (r: number, g: number, b: number) => {
    const ri = Math.min(5, Math.round((r * 5) / 255));
    const gi = Math.min(7, Math.round((g * 7) / 255));
    const bi = Math.min(4, Math.round((b * 4) / 255));
    return ri * 40 + gi * 5 + bi;
  };

  return { palette, mapRgb };
}

function lzwEncode(minCodeSize: number, indices: Uint8Array): number[] {
  const clearCode = 1 << minCodeSize;
  const eoiCode = clearCode + 1;
  let codeSize = minCodeSize + 1;
  let nextCode = eoiCode + 1;
  const maxTableSize = 4096;

  let dict = new Map<string, number>();
  const resetDict = () => {
    dict = new Map<string, number>();
    codeSize = minCodeSize + 1;
    nextCode = eoiCode + 1;
  };

  const outBytes: number[] = [];
  let bitBuffer = 0;
  let bitCount = 0;

  const emitCode = (code: number) => {
    bitBuffer |= code << bitCount;
    bitCount += codeSize;
    while (bitCount >= 8) {
      outBytes.push(bitBuffer & 0xff);
      bitBuffer >>= 8;
      bitCount -= 8;
    }
  };

  emitCode(clearCode);
  if (indices.length === 0) {
    emitCode(eoiCode);
    if (bitCount > 0) outBytes.push(bitBuffer & 0xff);
    return outBytes;
  }

  let prefix = String(indices[0]);
  for (let i = 1; i < indices.length; i++) {
    const c = indices[i];
    const key = `${prefix},${c}`;
    if (dict.has(key)) {
      prefix = key;
    } else {
      const prefixCode = prefix.includes(',') ? dict.get(prefix)! : Number(prefix);
      emitCode(prefixCode);
      if (nextCode < maxTableSize) {
        dict.set(key, nextCode++);
        if (nextCode > 1 << codeSize && codeSize < 12) {
          codeSize++;
        }
      } else {
        emitCode(clearCode);
        resetDict();
      }
      prefix = String(c);
    }
  }

  const finalCode = prefix.includes(',') ? dict.get(prefix)! : Number(prefix);
  emitCode(finalCode);
  emitCode(eoiCode);
  if (bitCount > 0) {
    outBytes.push(bitBuffer & 0xff);
  }
  return outBytes;
}

export function encodeAnimatedGif(
  width: number,
  height: number,
  frames: GifFrameInput[],
  loop = true
): Uint8Array {
  const { palette, mapRgb } = buildUniformPalette();
  const bytes: number[] = [];

  const pushStr = (s: string) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) bytes.push(s.charCodeAt(i));
  };
  const pushU16 = (v: number) => {
    bytes.push(v & 0xff, (v >> 8) & 0xff);
  };

  // Header
  pushStr('GIF89a');
  // Logical Screen Descriptor
  pushU16(width);
  pushU16(height);
  bytes.push(0xf7); // Global color table flag (1), color res (7), sort (0), size (7 -> 256 colors)
  bytes.push(0x00); // Background color index
  bytes.push(0x00); // Pixel aspect ratio

  // Global Color Table (256 * 3 = 768 bytes)
  for (let i = 0; i < palette.length; i++) {
    bytes.push(palette[i]);
  }

  // Netscape 2.0 looping extension
  if (loop) {
    bytes.push(0x21, 0xff, 0x0b);
    pushStr('NETSCAPE2.0');
    bytes.push(0x03, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00);
  }

  for (const frame of frames) {
    // Graphic Control Extension
    bytes.push(0x21, 0xf9, 0x04, 0x04); // dispose=1 (do not dispose), no transparency
    pushU16(Math.max(2, Math.round(frame.delayCs)));
    bytes.push(0x00, 0x00);

    // Image Descriptor
    bytes.push(0x2c);
    pushU16(0);
    pushU16(0);
    pushU16(width);
    pushU16(height);
    bytes.push(0x00); // no local color table

    // Quantize pixels
    const pixelCount = width * height;
    const indices = new Uint8Array(pixelCount);
    const rgba = frame.rgba;
    for (let p = 0; p < pixelCount; p++) {
      const off = p * 4;
      const a = rgba[off + 3] / 255;
      // Blend over dark charcoal or white if semi-transparent
      const r = Math.round(rgba[off] * a + 23 * (1 - a));
      const g = Math.round(rgba[off + 1] * a + 25 * (1 - a));
      const b = Math.round(rgba[off + 2] * a + 29 * (1 - a));
      indices[p] = mapRgb(r, g, b);
    }

    const minCodeSize = 8;
    bytes.push(minCodeSize);
    const compressed = lzwEncode(minCodeSize, indices);

    // Emit sub-blocks (max 255 bytes each)
    let pos = 0;
    while (pos < compressed.length) {
      const chunkLen = Math.min(255, compressed.length - pos);
      bytes.push(chunkLen);
      for (let i = 0; i < chunkLen; i++) {
        bytes.push(compressed[pos + i]);
      }
      pos += chunkLen;
    }
    bytes.push(0x00); // Block terminator
  }

  bytes.push(0x3b); // GIF Trailer
  return new Uint8Array(bytes);
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/engine/psdImport.ts
   ============================================================================ */

/**
 * Lightweight browser-native PSD parser for layered 8-bit RGB/RGBA PSD import.
 * Extracts layer names, bounds, visibility, opacity, and uncompressed or PackBits RLE pixel channels.
 */

export interface ParsedPsdLayer {
  name: string;
  top: number;
  left: number;
  bottom: number;
  right: number;
  opacity: number;
  visible: boolean;
  rgba: Uint8ClampedArray;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export interface ParsedPsd {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  layers: ParsedPsdLayer[];
}

function decodePackBits(src: Uint8Array, expectedLen: number): Uint8Array {
  const out = new Uint8Array(expectedLen);
  let i = 0;
  let o = 0;
  while (i < src.length && o < expectedLen) {
    const n = (src[i++] << 24) >> 24; // signed int8
    if (n >= 0) {
      const count = n + 1;
      for (let k = 0; k < count && i < src.length && o < expectedLen; k++) {
        out[o++] = src[i++];
      }
    } else if (n !== -128) {
      const count = 1 - n;
      const val = src[i++] ?? 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < count && o < expectedLen; k++) {
        out[o++] = val;
      }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function parsePsdBuffer(buffer: ArrayBuffer): ParsedPsd {
  const view = new DataView(buffer);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  let offset = 0;

  const sig = String.fromCharCode(bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]);
  if (sig !== '8BPS') {
    throw new Error('Not a valid PSD file (missing 8BPS signature)');
  }
  offset += 4;
  const version = view.getUint16(offset);
  offset += 2;
  if (version !== 1) {
    throw new Error('Only standard PSD (version 1) files are supported');
  }
  offset += 6; // reserved
  const channels = view.getUint16(offset);
  offset += 2;
  const height = view.getUint32(offset);
  offset += 4;
  const width = view.getUint32(offset);
  offset += 4;
  const depth = view.getUint16(offset);
  offset += 2;
  const colorMode = view.getUint16(offset);
  offset += 2;

  if (depth !== 8 || (colorMode !== 3 && colorMode !== 1)) {
    throw new Error('Only 8-bit RGB or Grayscale PSD files are supported for layered import');
  }

  // Color mode data section
  const colorModeLen = view.getUint32(offset);
  offset += 4 + colorModeLen;

  // Image resources section
  const resourcesLen = view.getUint32(offset);
  offset += 4 + resourcesLen;

  // Layer and mask information section
  const layerMaskLen = view.getUint32(offset);
  offset += 4;

  const layers: ParsedPsdLayer[] = [];

  if (layerMaskLen > 0) {
    const layerInfoLen = view.getUint32(offset);
    offset += 4;
    if (layerInfoLen > 0) {
      let layerCount = view.getInt16(offset);
      offset += 2;
      if (layerCount < 0) layerCount = Math.abs(layerCount);

      interface RawLayerMeta {
        name: string;
        top: number;
        left: number;
        bottom: number;
        right: number;
        opacity: number;
        visible: boolean;
        channels: { id: number; length: number }[];
      }

      const metas: RawLayerMeta[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < layerCount; i++) {
        const top = view.getInt32(offset);
        const left = view.getInt32(offset + 4);
        const bottom = view.getInt32(offset + 8);
        const right = view.getInt32(offset + 12);
        offset += 16;

        const chCount = view.getUint16(offset);
        offset += 2;
        const chInfo: { id: number; length: number }[] = [];
        for (let c = 0; c < chCount; c++) {
          const id = view.getInt16(offset);
          const length = view.getUint32(offset + 2);
          offset += 6;
          chInfo.push({ id, length });
        }

        // Blend mode sig '8BIM' + key (4) + opacity (1) + clipping (1) + flags (1) + filler (1)
        offset += 8;
        const opacity = bytes[offset++];
        offset++; // clipping
        const flags = bytes[offset++];
        offset++; // filler
        const visible = (flags & 0x02) === 0;

        const extraLen = view.getUint32(offset);
        offset += 4;
        const extraEnd = offset + extraLen;

        // Layer mask data
        const maskDataLen = view.getUint32(offset);
        offset += 4 + maskDataLen;
        // Blending ranges
        const blendRangesLen = view.getUint32(offset);
        offset += 4 + blendRangesLen;
        // Pascal string name padded to multiple of 4
        const nameLen = bytes[offset++];
        let name = '';
        for (let n = 0; n < nameLen; n++) {
          name += String.fromCharCode(bytes[offset + n]);
        }
        offset = extraEnd;

        metas.push({
          name: name.trim() || `PSD Layer ${i + 1}`,
          top,
          left,
          bottom,
          right,
          opacity: opacity / 255,
          visible,
          channels: chInfo,
        });
      }

      for (const meta of metas) {
        const lw = Math.max(0, meta.right - meta.left);
        const lh = Math.max(0, meta.bottom - meta.top);
        const channelData = new Map<number, Uint8Array>();

        for (const ch of meta.channels) {
          const chEnd = offset + ch.length;
          if (lw > 0 && lh > 0 && ch.length >= 2) {
            const compression = view.getUint16(offset);
            const rawStart = offset + 2;
            if (compression === 0) {
              channelData.set(ch.id, bytes.slice(rawStart, rawStart + lw * lh));
            } else if (compression === 1) {
              // RLE PackBits: 2*lh bytes of row byte counts followed by RLE rows
              const rowCountsEnd = rawStart + lh * 2;
              const decoded = new Uint8Array(lw * lh);
              let rowPos = rowCountsEnd;
              for (let y = 0; y < lh; y++) {
                const rowLen = view.getUint16(rawStart + y * 2);
                const rowBytes = decodePackBits(bytes.slice(rowPos, rowPos + rowLen), lw);
                decoded.set(rowBytes, y * lw);
                rowPos += rowLen;
              }
              channelData.set(ch.id, decoded);
            }
          }
          offset = chEnd;
        }

        if (lw > 0 && lh > 0) {
          const rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(lw * lh * 4);
          const rCh = channelData.get(0);
          const gCh = channelData.get(1) ?? rCh;
          const bCh = channelData.get(2) ?? rCh;
          const aCh = channelData.get(-1);

          for (let p = 0; p < lw * lh; p++) {
            rgba[p * 4] = rCh ? rCh[p] : 0;
            rgba[p * 4 + 1] = gCh ? gCh[p] : 0;
            rgba[p * 4 + 2] = bCh ? bCh[p] : 0;
            rgba[p * 4 + 3] = aCh ? aCh[p] : 255;
          }

          layers.push({
            name: meta.name,
            top: meta.top,
            left: meta.left,
            bottom: meta.bottom,
            right: meta.right,
            opacity: meta.opacity,
            visible: meta.visible,
            rgba,
            width: lw,
            height: lh,
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Fallback if PSD has flattened composite only
  if (layers.length === 0 && channels >= 3) {
    // Minimal composite fallback
    return { width, height, layers: [] };
  }

  return { width, height, layers };
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/audio/audioEngine.ts
   ============================================================================ */

import type { AudioTrack, MouthShapeId } from '../engine/types';

const imageOrAudioCache = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();

let sharedAudioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;

function getAudioContext(): AudioContext | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  if (!sharedAudioCtx) {
    const Ctor =
      window.AudioContext ||
      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
    if (Ctor) {
      sharedAudioCtx = new Ctor();
    }
  }
  return sharedAudioCtx;
}

export async function decodeAudioFile(
  file: File
): Promise<{
  durationSec: number;
  peaks: number[];
  dataUrl: string;
}> {
  const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
  const dataUrl = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result));
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });

  const ctx = getAudioContext();
  if (!ctx) {
    return {
      durationSec: 3,
      peaks: Array.from({ length: 48 }, (_, i) => 0.2 + 0.6 * Math.abs(Math.sin(i * 0.45))),
      dataUrl,
    };
  }

  const audioBuffer = await ctx.decodeAudioData(arrayBuffer.slice(0));
  imageOrAudioCache.set(dataUrl, audioBuffer);

  const channel = audioBuffer.getChannelData(0);
  const barCount = Math.min(120, Math.max(24, Math.ceil(audioBuffer.duration * 12)));
  const blockSize = Math.max(1, Math.floor(channel.length / barCount));
  const peaks: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < barCount; i++) {
    const start = i * blockSize;
    let maxVal = 0;
    for (let j = 0; j < blockSize && start + j < channel.length; j++) {
      const v = Math.abs(channel[start + j]);
      if (v > maxVal) maxVal = v;
    }
    peaks.push(Math.min(1, maxVal));
  }

  return {
    durationSec: audioBuffer.duration,
    peaks,
    dataUrl,
  };
}

let activeSources: AudioBufferSourceNode[] = [];

export function stopAllAudioPlayback(): void {
  for (const src of activeSources) {
    try {
      src.stop();
    } catch {
      // Ignore already stopped
    }
  }
  activeSources = [];
}

export async function playTracksFromFrame(
  tracks: AudioTrack[],
  currentFrame: number,
  fps: number
): Promise<void> {
  stopAllAudioPlayback();
  const ctx = getAudioContext();
  if (!ctx) return;
  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
    await ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
  }

  const currentSec = currentFrame / fps;

  for (const track of tracks) {
    if (track.muted) continue;
    const trackStartSec = track.startFrame / fps;
    const offset = currentSec - trackStartSec;
    if (offset >= track.durationSec) continue;

    let buffer: AudioBuffer | undefined;
    if (track.dataUrl) {
      buffer = imageOrAudioCache.get(track.dataUrl);
      if (!buffer) {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(track.dataUrl);
          const ab = await res.arrayBuffer();
          buffer = await ctx.decodeAudioData(ab);
          imageOrAudioCache.set(track.dataUrl, buffer);
        } catch {
          continue;
        }
      }
    } else if (track.peaks.length > 0) {
      const cacheKey = `synth:${track.id}:${track.durationSec}:${track.peaks.join(',')}`;
      buffer = imageOrAudioCache.get(cacheKey);
      if (!buffer) {
        const sr = ctx.sampleRate || 22050;
        const len = Math.max(sr, Math.floor(track.durationSec * sr));
        const synth = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, sr);
        const ch = synth.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
          const t = i / sr;
          const idx = Math.min(
            track.peaks.length - 1,
            Math.floor((t / Math.max(0.1, track.durationSec)) * track.peaks.length)
          );
          const amp = track.peaks[idx] * 0.14;
          ch[i] = (Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 240 * t) * 0.65 + Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 480 * t) * 0.35) * amp;
        }
        buffer = synth;
        imageOrAudioCache.set(cacheKey, buffer);
      }
    }

    if (!buffer) continue;

    const source = ctx.createBufferSource();
    source.buffer = buffer;
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.value = track.volume;
    source.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);

    if (offset >= 0) {
      source.start(0, offset);
    } else {
      source.start(ctx.currentTime + Math.abs(offset), 0);
    }
    activeSources.push(source);
  }
}

/**
 * Play a short audio scrub blip or tone feedback when scrubbing dialogue tracks.
 */
export function scrubAudioFrame(tracks: AudioTrack[], frame: number, fps: number): void {
  const ctx = getAudioContext();
  if (!ctx) return;
  const hasActivePeak = tracks.some((t) => {
    if (t.muted) return false;
    const relFrame = frame - t.startFrame;
    if (relFrame < 0) return false;
    const idx = Math.floor((relFrame / fps / Math.max(0.1, t.durationSec)) * t.peaks.length);
    return (t.peaks[idx] ?? 0) > 0.22;
  });
  if (!hasActivePeak) return;

  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
    ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
  }

  const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  osc.type = 'sine';
  osc.frequency.value = 220 + (frame % 6) * 28;
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.03, ctx.currentTime);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + 0.045);
  osc.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc.start();
  osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.05);
}

/**
 * Renders a simple synthesized WAV file from project audio peaks or demo tone for WAV export.
 */
export function encodeTracksToWav(tracks: AudioTrack[], durationSec: number): Uint8Array {
  const sampleRate = 22050;
  const numSamples = Math.max(sampleRate, Math.floor(durationSec * sampleRate));
  const dataSize = numSamples * 2; // 16-bit mono
  const buffer = new ArrayBuffer(44 + dataSize);
  const view = new DataView(buffer);

  const writeStr = (off: number, s: string) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) view.setUint8(off + i, s.charCodeAt(i));
  };

  writeStr(0, 'RIFF');
  view.setUint32(4, 36 + dataSize, true);
  writeStr(8, 'WAVE');
  writeStr(12, 'fmt ');
  view.setUint32(16, 16, true);
  view.setUint16(20, 1, true); // PCM
  view.setUint16(22, 1, true); // mono
  view.setUint32(24, sampleRate, true);
  view.setUint32(28, sampleRate * 2, true);
  view.setUint16(32, 2, true);
  view.setUint16(34, 16, true);
  writeStr(36, 'data');
  view.setUint32(40, dataSize, true);

  for (let i = 0; i < numSamples; i++) {
    const t = i / sampleRate;
    let sample = 0;
    for (const tr of tracks) {
      if (tr.muted || tr.peaks.length === 0) continue;
      const idx = Math.min(tr.peaks.length - 1, Math.floor((t / Math.max(0.1, durationSec)) * tr.peaks.length));
      const amp = tr.peaks[idx] * tr.volume * 0.25;
      sample += Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * 260 * t) * amp;
    }
    const clamped = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, sample));
    view.setInt16(44 + i * 2, Math.floor(clamped * 32767), true);
  }

  return new Uint8Array(buffer);
}

import { resolveCameraAtFrame, solveForwardKinematics } from '../engine/math';
import {
  getOnionSkinNeighbors,
  resolveCelAtFrame,
  resolveLayerTransformAtFrame,
} from '../engine/timeline';
import type {
  BlendMode,
  CelFrame,
  CompositeEffect,
  GuideConfig,
  Layer,
  OnionSkinConfig,
  Scene,
  VectorStroke,
} from '../engine/types';

const imageCache = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();

export function getCachedImage(dataUrl: string, onLoad?: () => void): HTMLImageElement | null {
  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
  const cached = imageCache.get(dataUrl);
  if (cached) {
    return cached.complete ? cached : null;
  }
  const img = new Image();
  img.onload = () => {
    if (onLoad) onLoad();
  };
  img.src = dataUrl;
  imageCache.set(dataUrl, img);
  return img.complete ? img : null;
}

function mapBlendMode(mode: BlendMode): GlobalCompositeOperation {
  switch (mode) {
    case 'multiply':
      return 'multiply';
    case 'screen':
      return 'screen';
    case 'overlay':
      return 'overlay';
    case 'lighter':
      return 'lighter';
    case 'darken':
      return 'darken';
    case 'lighten':
      return 'lighten';
    default:
      return 'source-over';
  }
}

export function drawVectorStroke(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  stroke: VectorStroke
): void {
  const pts = stroke.points;
  if (pts.length === 0) return;

  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.02, Math.min(1, stroke.opacity));
  if (stroke.tool === 'eraser') {
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
  } else {
    ctx.strokeStyle = stroke.color;
    ctx.fillStyle = stroke.color;
  }

  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.lineJoin = 'round';

  if (stroke.tool === 'text' && stroke.text) {
    const p = pts[0];
    ctx.font = `600 ${Math.max(12, stroke.size * 3.5)}px "Plus Jakarta Sans", sans-serif`;
    ctx.fillText(stroke.text, p.x, p.y);
    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }

  if (stroke.tool === 'line' && pts.length >= 2) {
    const a = pts[0];
    const b = pts[pts.length - 1];
    ctx.lineWidth = stroke.size;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y);
    ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }

  if (stroke.tool === 'rect' && pts.length >= 2) {
    const a = pts[0];
    const b = pts[pts.length - 1];
    const x = Math.min(a.x, b.x);
    const y = Math.min(a.y, b.y);
    const w = Math.abs(b.x - a.x);
    const h = Math.abs(b.y - a.y);
    ctx.lineWidth = stroke.size;
    if (stroke.fill) {
      ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
    } else {
      ctx.strokeRect(x, y, w, h);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }

  if (stroke.tool === 'ellipse' && pts.length >= 2) {
    const a = pts[0];
    const b = pts[pts.length - 1];
    const cx = (a.x + b.x) / 2;
    const cy = (a.y + b.y) / 2;
    const rx = Math.max(1, Math.abs(b.x - a.x) / 2);
    const ry = Math.max(1, Math.abs(b.y - a.y) / 2);
    ctx.lineWidth = stroke.size;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, rx, ry, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    if (stroke.fill) {
      ctx.fill();
    } else {
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }

  // Freehand stroke (pencil / brush / pen / eraser across 31 brush physics types)
  const isCloud = stroke.texture.startsWith('cloud-');

  if (isCloud) {
    const isEraser = stroke.tool === 'eraser';
    interface CloudLobe {
      x: number;
      y: number;
      r: number;
      angle: number;
      isTop?: boolean;
    }

    const lobes: CloudLobe[] = [];
    const baseSize = Math.max(6, stroke.size);
    const sampleStep = Math.max(7, baseSize * (stroke.texture === 'cloud-cirrus' ? 0.34 : 0.44));

    const pushPuffCluster = (cx: number, cy: number, pScale: number, angle: number, idx: number) => {
      const r = Math.max(4, baseSize * (0.35 + pScale * 0.48));
      // Multi-harmonic size modulation creates large cumulus peaks & smaller valleys
      const wave = 0.55 + 0.85 * Math.abs(Math.sin(idx * 1.15 + 0.4));
      const domeR = r * wave;

      if (stroke.texture === 'cloud-anime-cel' || stroke.texture === 'cloud-cumulus') {
        const lift = Math.abs(Math.sin(idx * 1.65)) * r * 0.52;
        lobes.push({ x: cx, y: cy - lift * 0.65, r: domeR, angle, isTop: true });
        lobes.push({
          x: cx + Math.cos(idx * 2.7) * r * 0.5,
          y: cy + r * 0.14,
          r: r * (0.58 + (idx % 3) * 0.12),
          angle,
        });
        if (idx % 2 === 0) {
          lobes.push({
            x: cx - Math.sin(idx * 2.1) * r * 0.48,
            y: cy - lift * 0.9 - r * 0.15,
            r: domeR * 0.68,
            angle,
            isTop: true,
          });
        }
      } else if (stroke.texture === 'cloud-nimbus') {
        const lift = Math.abs(Math.cos(idx * 1.3)) * r * 0.42;
        lobes.push({ x: cx, y: cy - lift * 0.45, r: domeR * 1.08, angle, isTop: true });
        lobes.push({
          x: cx + Math.sin(idx * 2.3) * r * 0.65,
          y: cy + r * 0.24,
          r: r * 0.72,
          angle,
        });
      } else {
        // cloud-cirrus or cloud-mist
        lobes.push({
          x: cx + Math.cos(idx * 2.9) * r * 0.25,
          y: cy + Math.sin(idx * 2.3) * r * 0.22,
          r: domeR,
          angle,
        });
      }
    };

    if (pts.length === 1) {
      pushPuffCluster(pts[0].x, pts[0].y, pts[0].pressure, 0, 1);
      pushPuffCluster(pts[0].x + baseSize * 0.36, pts[0].y + baseSize * 0.08, pts[0].pressure * 0.85, 0, 2);
      pushPuffCluster(pts[0].x - baseSize * 0.34, pts[0].y + baseSize * 0.1, pts[0].pressure * 0.8, 0, 3);
    } else {
      let carry = 0;
      let sampleIdx = 0;
      pushPuffCluster(pts[0].x, pts[0].y, pts[0].pressure, Math.atan2(pts[1].y - pts[0].y, pts[1].x - pts[0].x), 0);
      for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
        const a = pts[i - 1];
        const b = pts[i];
        const dx = b.x - a.x;
        const dy = b.y - a.y;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist < 0.01) continue;
        const angle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
        let d = sampleStep - carry;
        while (d <= dist) {
          const t = d / dist;
          sampleIdx++;
          pushPuffCluster(
            a.x + dx * t,
            a.y + dy * t,
            a.pressure + (b.pressure - a.pressure) * t,
            angle,
            sampleIdx
          );
          d += sampleStep;
        }
        carry = dist - (d - sampleStep);
      }
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'cloud-anime-cel') {
      // Pass 1: Whole crisp underside cel shadow silhouette (no overlapping internal scales)
      if (!isEraser) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(72, 102, 162, 0.36)';
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity;
        for (const l of lobes) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(l.x + l.r * 0.08, l.y + l.r * 0.24, l.r * 0.98, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.restore();
      }
      // Pass 2: Main solid crisp cel cloud silhouette
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      ctx.fillStyle = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity;
      for (const l of lobes) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(l.x, l.y, l.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Pass 3: Bright sunlit upper rim peaks
      if (!isEraser) {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)';
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.85;
        for (const l of lobes) {
          if (!l.isTop) continue;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(l.x - l.r * 0.08, l.y - l.r * 0.16, l.r * 0.72, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'cloud-cumulus') {
      // Pass 1: Soft underside atmospheric shadow
      if (!isEraser) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(85, 112, 165, 0.32)';
        ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(6, baseSize * 0.28);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(85, 112, 165, 0.28)';
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.75;
        for (const l of lobes) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.ellipse(l.x + l.r * 0.05, l.y + l.r * 0.26, l.r * 1.02, l.r * 0.72, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.restore();
      }
      // Pass 2: Soft billowing main cloud domes
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(5, baseSize * 0.26);
      ctx.fillStyle = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.84;
      for (const l of lobes) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(l.x, l.y, l.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Pass 3: Sunlit top dome highlights
      if (!isEraser) {
        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
        ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(4, baseSize * 0.2);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)';
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.8;
        for (const l of lobes) {
          if (!l.isTop) continue;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(l.x - l.r * 0.06, l.y - l.r * 0.18, l.r * 0.68, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'cloud-nimbus') {
      // Pass 1: Heavy flat-bottomed dark storm cloud bank
      if (!isEraser) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(14, 18, 30, 0.45)';
        ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(8, baseSize * 0.35);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(16, 22, 36, 0.48)';
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.92;
        for (const l of lobes) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.ellipse(l.x, l.y + l.r * 0.32, l.r * 1.15, l.r * 0.56, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.restore();
      }
      // Pass 2: Dense turbulent upper cloud volumes
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(6, baseSize * 0.32);
      ctx.fillStyle = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.8;
      for (const l of lobes) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(l.x, l.y - l.r * 0.08, l.r * 0.96, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Pass 3: Subtle rim catchlight on highest storm domes
      if (!isEraser) {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(210, 225, 245, 0.16)';
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.7;
        for (const l of lobes) {
          if (!l.isTop) continue;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(l.x, l.y - l.r * 0.25, l.r * 0.55, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'cloud-cirrus') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(3, baseSize * 0.18);
      for (let idx = 0; idx < lobes.length; idx++) {
        const l = lobes[idx];
        for (let w = 0; w < 3; w++) {
          const spread = (w - 1) * l.r * 0.36;
          const tilt = l.angle + Math.sin(idx * 1.9 + w) * 0.15;
          const px = l.x - Math.sin(l.angle) * spread;
          const py = l.y + Math.cos(l.angle) * spread;
          ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * (0.24 + (w % 2) * 0.14);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.ellipse(
            px,
            py,
            l.r * (1.45 + (w % 2) * 0.45),
            Math.max(1.4, l.r * 0.16),
            tilt,
            0,
            Math.PI * 2
          );
          ctx.fill();
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    } else {
      // cloud-mist: smooth horizontal atmospheric haze
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = isEraser ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
      ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(8, baseSize * 0.42);
      for (let idx = 0; idx < lobes.length; idx++) {
        const l = lobes[idx];
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.22;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(l.x, l.y, l.r * 1.75, l.r * 0.48, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }

  if (pts.length === 1) {
    const p = pts[0];
    const r = Math.max(0.8, (stroke.size * p.pressure) / 2);
    if (stroke.texture === 'pixel') {
      const s = Math.max(2, Math.round(stroke.size));
      ctx.fillRect(Math.floor(p.x / s) * s, Math.floor(p.y / s) * s, s, s);
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'sparkle') {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y - r * 1.6);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x + r * 0.4, p.y - r * 0.4);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x + r * 1.6, p.y);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x + r * 0.4, p.y + r * 0.4);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y + r * 1.6);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x - r * 0.4, p.y + r * 0.4);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x - r * 1.6, p.y);
      ctx.lineTo(p.x - r * 0.4, p.y - r * 0.4);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
    } else {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    return;
  }

  if (stroke.texture === 'glow' && stroke.tool !== 'eraser') {
    ctx.shadowColor = stroke.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(8, stroke.size * 1.4);
  } else if (stroke.texture === 'airbrush' || stroke.texture === 'smudge-soft') {
    ctx.shadowColor = stroke.tool === 'eraser' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,1)' : stroke.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = Math.max(8, stroke.size * 0.9);
  } else if (stroke.hardness < 0.7 && stroke.tool === 'brush') {
    ctx.shadowColor = stroke.color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = (1 - stroke.hardness) * stroke.size * 0.85;
  }

  if (stroke.texture === 'marker' || stroke.texture === 'highlighter' || stroke.texture === 'palette-knife') {
    ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'bevel';
  }

  if (stroke.texture === 'dashed') {
    ctx.setLineDash([Math.max(4, stroke.size * 2), Math.max(4, stroke.size * 1.6)]);
  } else if (stroke.texture === 'dotted') {
    ctx.setLineDash([0.5, Math.max(4, stroke.size * 1.75)]);
  }

  const halftoneSeen = stroke.texture === 'halftone' ? new Set<string>() : null;

  for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
    const prev = pts[i - 1];
    const curr = pts[i];
    const pressure = (prev.pressure + curr.pressure) / 2;
    let width = Math.max(0.75, stroke.size * (0.35 + pressure * 0.85));

    if (stroke.texture === 'pixel') {
      const cell = Math.max(2, Math.round(stroke.size));
      const steps = Math.max(
        1,
        Math.ceil(Math.hypot(curr.x - prev.x, curr.y - prev.y) / (cell * 0.5))
      );
      for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {
        const px = prev.x + ((curr.x - prev.x) * s) / steps;
        const py = prev.y + ((curr.y - prev.y) * s) / steps;
        ctx.fillRect(Math.floor(px / cell) * cell, Math.floor(py / cell) * cell, cell, cell);
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'halftone' && halftoneSeen) {
      const step = Math.max(5, Math.round(stroke.size * 0.34));
      const radius = Math.max(step, stroke.size * (0.45 + pressure * 0.45));
      const x0 = Math.floor((curr.x - radius) / step) * step;
      const x1 = Math.ceil((curr.x + radius) / step) * step;
      const y0 = Math.floor((curr.y - radius) / step) * step;
      const y1 = Math.ceil((curr.y + radius) / step) * step;
      for (let gx = x0; gx <= x1; gx += step) {
        for (let gy = y0; gy <= y1; gy += step) {
          const dist = Math.hypot(gx - curr.x, gy - curr.y);
          if (dist <= radius) {
            const key = `${gx},${gy}`;
            if (halftoneSeen.has(key)) continue;
            halftoneSeen.add(key);
            const dotR = Math.max(1.1, step * 0.34 * (1 - (dist / radius) * 0.45));
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(gx, gy, dotR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
          }
        }
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'sparkle') {
