      const segDist = Math.hypot(curr.x - prev.x, curr.y - prev.y);
      if (i % 2 === 1 || segDist > stroke.size * 0.7) {
        const starR = Math.max(3, width * (0.45 + ((i * 37) % 10) * 0.06));
        const ox = Math.sin(i * 13.1) * width * 0.4;
        const oy = Math.cos(i * 17.7) * width * 0.4;
        const sx = curr.x + ox;
        const sy = curr.y + oy;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(sx, sy - starR);
        ctx.lineTo(sx + starR * 0.24, sy - starR * 0.24);
        ctx.lineTo(sx + starR, sy);
        ctx.lineTo(sx + starR * 0.24, sy + starR * 0.24);
        ctx.lineTo(sx, sy + starR);
        ctx.lineTo(sx - starR * 0.24, sy + starR * 0.24);
        ctx.lineTo(sx - starR, sy);
        ctx.lineTo(sx - starR * 0.24, sy - starR * 0.24);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.55;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(sx - ox * 1.4, sy - oy * 1.4, Math.max(1, starR * 0.16), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'spray') {
      const radius = Math.max(4, stroke.size * 0.9);
      const count = Math.max(5, Math.round(stroke.size * 0.5));
      for (let d = 0; d < count; d++) {
        const a = ((i * 73 + d * 137) % 360) * (Math.PI / 180);
        const dist = (((i * 29 + d * 53) % 100) / 100) * radius;
        const dotR = 0.8 + (d % 3) * 0.7;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(curr.x + Math.cos(a) * dist, curr.y + Math.sin(a) * dist, dotR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'chalk') {
      const radius = Math.max(4, width * 0.65);
      const grains = Math.max(8, Math.round(width * 0.7));
      ctx.save();
      for (let g = 0; g < grains; g++) {
        const a = ((i * 47 + g * 97) % 360) * (Math.PI / 180);
        const d = (((i * 31 + g * 67) % 100) / 100) * radius;
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * (0.12 + (g % 4) * 0.08);
        ctx.fillRect(curr.x + Math.cos(a) * d, curr.y + Math.sin(a) * d, 2.5, 2.5);
      }
      ctx.restore();
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'crosshatch') {
      const len = Math.max(6, width * 0.75);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, stroke.size * 0.09);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(curr.x - len * 0.5, curr.y - len * 0.5);
      ctx.lineTo(curr.x + len * 0.5, curr.y + len * 0.5);
      if (i % 2 === 0) {
        ctx.moveTo(curr.x - len * 0.5, curr.y + len * 0.5);
        ctx.lineTo(curr.x + len * 0.5, curr.y - len * 0.5);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'fur') {
      const angle = Math.atan2(curr.y - prev.y, curr.x - prev.x);
      const perp = angle + Math.PI / 2;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.9, stroke.size * 0.09);
      for (let f = -2; f <= 2; f++) {
        const off = f * (width * 0.2);
        const tilt = angle + f * 0.22 + Math.sin(i + f) * 0.12;
        const bx = curr.x + Math.cos(perp) * off;
        const by = curr.y + Math.sin(perp) * off;
        const hairLen = width * (0.55 + (Math.abs(f) % 2) * 0.25);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(bx, by);
        ctx.lineTo(bx + Math.cos(tilt) * hairLen, by + Math.sin(tilt) * hairLen);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'fountain') {
      const progress = i / Math.max(1, pts.length - 1);
      const endTaper = Math.min(1, progress * 5, (1 - progress) * 5 + 0.15);
      width = Math.max(0.6, stroke.size * (0.25 + pressure * 0.9) * endTaper);
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'pencil-hb') {
      width = Math.max(0.65, stroke.size * (0.3 + pressure * 0.65));
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'calligraphy') {
      const segAngle = Math.atan2(curr.y - prev.y, curr.x - prev.x);
      const nibAngle = -Math.PI / 4;
      const factor = 0.25 + 0.95 * Math.abs(Math.sin(segAngle - nibAngle));
      width = Math.max(1, stroke.size * factor * (0.5 + pressure * 0.6));
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'highlighter') {
      width = Math.max(4, stroke.size * 1.15);
    } else if (stroke.texture === 'palette-knife') {
      const segAngle = Math.atan2(curr.y - prev.y, curr.x - prev.x);
      const bladeFactor = 0.35 + 0.85 * Math.abs(Math.cos(segAngle - Math.PI / 6));
      width = Math.max(2, stroke.size * bladeFactor);
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'gouache' || stroke.texture === 'oil-impasto') {
      const segAngle = Math.atan2(curr.y - prev.y, curr.x - prev.x);
      const perp = segAngle + Math.PI / 2;
      const strands = 5;
      ctx.save();
      for (let b = 0; b < strands; b++) {
        const ratio = (b / (strands - 1) - 0.5) * 0.82;
        const off = ratio * width;
        const ox = Math.cos(perp) * off;
        const oy = Math.sin(perp) * off;
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1.2, width * (stroke.texture === 'oil-impasto' ? 0.32 : 0.24));
        ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * (0.72 + (b % 2) * 0.22);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(prev.x + ox, prev.y + oy);
        ctx.lineTo(curr.x + ox, curr.y + oy);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      if (stroke.texture === 'oil-impasto') {
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)';
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, width * 0.14);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(prev.x + Math.cos(perp) * width * 0.28, prev.y + Math.sin(perp) * width * 0.28);
        ctx.lineTo(curr.x + Math.cos(perp) * width * 0.28, curr.y + Math.sin(perp) * width * 0.28);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
      continue;
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'ribbon') {
      const segAngle = Math.atan2(curr.y - prev.y, curr.x - prev.x);
      const perp = segAngle + Math.PI / 2;
      const halfW = width * 0.5;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.fillStyle = stroke.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(prev.x + Math.cos(perp) * halfW, prev.y + Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.lineTo(curr.x + Math.cos(perp) * halfW, curr.y + Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.lineTo(curr.x - Math.cos(perp) * halfW, curr.y - Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.lineTo(prev.x - Math.cos(perp) * halfW, prev.y - Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.25;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(prev.x + Math.cos(perp) * halfW, prev.y + Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.lineTo(curr.x + Math.cos(perp) * halfW, curr.y + Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.moveTo(prev.x - Math.cos(perp) * halfW, prev.y - Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.lineTo(curr.x - Math.cos(perp) * halfW, curr.y - Math.sin(perp) * halfW);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
      continue;
    }

    ctx.lineWidth = width;
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (i < pts.length - 1) {
      const next = pts[i + 1];
      const mx = (curr.x + next.x) / 2;
      const my = (curr.y + next.y) / 2;
      ctx.moveTo(prev.x, prev.y);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(curr.x, curr.y, mx, my);
    } else {
      ctx.moveTo(prev.x, prev.y);
      ctx.lineTo(curr.x, curr.y);
    }
    ctx.stroke();

    if (stroke.texture === 'glow' && stroke.tool !== 'eraser') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, width * 0.32);
      ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'watercolor') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.18;
      ctx.lineWidth = width * 1.45;
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'smudge-soft') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.25;
      ctx.lineWidth = width * 1.6;
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }

    if ((stroke.texture === 'charcoal' || stroke.texture === 'pencil-hb') && i % 2 === 0) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * (stroke.texture === 'pencil-hb' ? 0.14 : 0.25);
      const jitterX = Math.sin(i * 19.3) * (width * 0.45);
      const jitterY = Math.cos(i * 11.7) * (width * 0.45);
      ctx.fillRect(curr.x + jitterX, curr.y + jitterY, 1.5, 1.5);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'crayon') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = stroke.opacity * 0.35;
      for (let g = 0; g < 3; g++) {
        const gx = Math.sin(i * 13.7 + g * 5.1) * width * 0.45;
        const gy = Math.cos(i * 9.2 + g * 7.3) * width * 0.45;
        ctx.fillRect(curr.x + gx, curr.y + gy, 2.2, 2.2);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    if (stroke.texture === 'ink-grain' && i % 3 === 0) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(curr.x, curr.y, Math.max(0.8, width * 0.18), 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  ctx.restore();
}

export function drawCelContent(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  cel: CelFrame,
  onImageLoad?: () => void
): void {
  if (cel.rasterDataUrl) {
    const img = getCachedImage(cel.rasterDataUrl, onImageLoad);
    if (img) {
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
    }
  }
  for (const stroke of cel.strokes) {
    drawVectorStroke(ctx, stroke);
  }
}

function drawTintedCel(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  cel: CelFrame,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  tintColor: string,
  opacity: number,
  onImageLoad?: () => void
): void {
  const scratch = document.createElement('canvas');
  scratch.width = width;
  scratch.height = height;
  const sctx = scratch.getContext('2d');
  if (!sctx) return;

  drawCelContent(sctx, cel, onImageLoad);
  sctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-in';
  sctx.fillStyle = tintColor;
  sctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = opacity;
  ctx.drawImage(scratch, 0, 0);
  ctx.restore();
}

function applyCompositingEffects(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  effects: CompositeEffect[],
  frame: number
): void {
  const enabled = effects.filter((e) => e.enabled);
  if (enabled.length === 0) return;

  // Color grade / blur via canvas filter pass
  const colorGrade = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'color-grade');
  const gBlur = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'gaussian-blur');
  const mBlur = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'motion-blur');

  if (colorGrade || gBlur) {
    const snap = document.createElement('canvas');
    snap.width = width;
    snap.height = height;
    const sctx = snap.getContext('2d');
    if (sctx) {
      sctx.drawImage(ctx.canvas, 0, 0);
      ctx.save();
      const filters: string[] = [];
      if (gBlur) filters.push(`blur(${Math.max(0.5, gBlur.radius * gBlur.intensity)}px)`);
      if (colorGrade) {
        const b = 100 + (colorGrade.brightness ?? 0);
        const c = 100 + (colorGrade.contrast ?? 0);
        const s = 100 + (colorGrade.saturation ?? 0);
        filters.push(`brightness(${b}%) contrast(${c}%) saturate(${s}%)`);
      }
      ctx.filter = filters.join(' ');
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
      ctx.drawImage(snap, 0, 0);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  if (mBlur) {
    const snap = document.createElement('canvas');
    snap.width = width;
    snap.height = height;
    const sctx = snap.getContext('2d');
    if (sctx) {
      sctx.drawImage(ctx.canvas, 0, 0);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = mBlur.intensity * 0.25;
      for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
        ctx.drawImage(snap, -i * (mBlur.radius * 0.4), 0);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  const animeGlow = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'anime-glow');
  const lightBloom = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'light-bloom');
  if (animeGlow || lightBloom) {
    const fx = animeGlow ?? lightBloom!;
    const glowCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    glowCanvas.width = width;
    glowCanvas.height = height;
    const gctx = glowCanvas.getContext('2d');
    if (gctx) {
      gctx.filter = `blur(${Math.max(4, fx.radius)}px)`;
      gctx.drawImage(ctx.canvas, 0, 0);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
      ctx.globalAlpha = fx.intensity * 0.45;
      ctx.drawImage(glowCanvas, 0, 0);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  const speedLines = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'speed-lines');
  if (speedLines) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = speedLines.color ?? '#FFFFFF';
    ctx.globalAlpha = speedLines.intensity * 0.55;
    const cx = width / 2;
    const cy = height / 2;
    const count = 44;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const a = (i / count) * Math.PI * 2 + (frame % 3) * 0.08;
      const rIn = Math.min(width, height) * (0.28 + ((i * 37) % 15) * 0.015);
      const rOut = Math.max(width, height) * 0.8;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1 + (i % 3);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * rIn, cy + Math.sin(a) * rIn);
      ctx.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(a) * rOut, cy + Math.sin(a) * rOut);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  const particles = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'particles');
  if (particles) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = particles.color ?? '#64D6C5';
    for (let i = 0; i < 28; i++) {
      const px = ((Math.sin(i * 17.3 + frame * 0.25) + 1) * 0.5) * width;
      const py = ((Math.cos(i * 23.1 - frame * 0.35) + 1) * 0.5) * height;
      const r = 1.5 + (i % 4) * 1.1;
      ctx.globalAlpha = particles.intensity * (0.3 + (i % 5) * 0.14);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  const impactFlash = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'impact-flash');
  if (impactFlash) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = impactFlash.color ?? '#FFFFFF';
    ctx.globalAlpha = impactFlash.intensity * 0.35;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  const vignette = enabled.find((e) => e.type === 'vignette');
  if (vignette) {
    ctx.save();
    const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(
      width / 2,
      height / 2,
      Math.min(width, height) * 0.25,
      width / 2,
      height / 2,
      Math.max(width, height) * 0.72
    );
    grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    grad.addColorStop(1, `rgba(9,10,13,${Math.min(0.85, vignette.intensity * 1.4)})`);
    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

export interface RenderSceneOptions {
  frame: number;
  activeLayerId?: string;
  onionSkin?: OnionSkinConfig;
  guides?: GuideConfig;
  showRig?: boolean;
  selectedBoneId?: string | null;
  applyCamera?: boolean;
  applyEffects?: boolean;
  showGuides?: boolean;
  skipBackground?: boolean;
  liveStroke?: VectorStroke | null;
  onImageLoad?: () => void;
}

export function renderSceneToCanvas(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  scene: Scene,
  options: RenderSceneOptions
): void {
  const { width, height } = scene;
  const {
    frame,
    activeLayerId,
    onionSkin,
    guides,
    showRig = false,
    selectedBoneId = null,
    applyCamera = true,
    applyEffects = true,
    showGuides = true,
    skipBackground = false,
    liveStroke = null,
    onImageLoad,
  } = options;

  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  if (!scene.transparentBg && !skipBackground) {
    ctx.fillStyle = scene.bgColor || '#1B1E26';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
  }

  const cam = applyCamera
    ? resolveCameraAtFrame(scene.cameraKeyframes, frame)
    : { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1, rotation: 0, shakeOffsetX: 0, shakeOffsetY: 0 };

  const renderLayerWithContent = (
    targetCtx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
    layer: Layer
  ) => {
    const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(layer, frame);
    const tf = resolveLayerTransformAtFrame(layer.keyframes, frame);

    targetCtx.save();
    targetCtx.globalAlpha = layer.opacity * tf.opacity;
    targetCtx.globalCompositeOperation = mapBlendMode(layer.blendMode);

    // Apply Camera + Multiplane Parallax + Layer Keyframe Transform
    targetCtx.translate(width / 2, height / 2);
    if (applyCamera) {
      targetCtx.scale(cam.zoom, cam.zoom);
      targetCtx.rotate((cam.rotation * Math.PI) / 180);
      targetCtx.translate(
        (-cam.x + cam.shakeOffsetX) * layer.parallaxDepth,
        (-cam.y + cam.shakeOffsetY) * layer.parallaxDepth
      );
    }
    targetCtx.translate(-width / 2, -height / 2);

    // Layer transform around center
    targetCtx.translate(width / 2 + tf.x, height / 2 + tf.y);
    targetCtx.rotate((tf.rotation * Math.PI) / 180);
    targetCtx.scale(tf.scaleX, tf.scaleY);
    targetCtx.translate(-width / 2, -height / 2);

    // Apply Skeletal Bone Binding transform if a bone is bound to this layer
    const boundBone = scene.rig.bones.find((b) => b.boundLayerId === layer.id);
    if (boundBone) {
      const worldBones = solveForwardKinematics(scene.rig.bones);
      const wb = worldBones[boundBone.id];
      if (wb) {
        targetCtx.translate(wb.startX, wb.startY);
        targetCtx.rotate(((wb.worldAngle + 90) * Math.PI) / 180);
        targetCtx.translate(-wb.startX, -wb.startY);
      }
    }

    // Onion skinning (only on active cel layer when editing)
    if (onionSkin?.enabled && layer.id === activeLayerId) {
      const { prev, next } = getOnionSkinNeighbors(
        layer,
        frame,
        onionSkin.prevFrames,
        onionSkin.nextFrames
      );
      for (const item of prev) {
        const alpha = onionSkin.opacity / item.distance;
        drawTintedCel(
          targetCtx,
          item.cel,
          width,
          height,
          onionSkin.prevColor,
          alpha,
          onImageLoad
        );
      }
      for (const item of next) {
        const alpha = onionSkin.opacity / item.distance;
        drawTintedCel(
          targetCtx,
          item.cel,
          width,
          height,
          onionSkin.nextColor,
          alpha,
          onImageLoad
        );
      }
    }

    if (cel) {
      drawCelContent(targetCtx, cel, onImageLoad);
    }
    if (liveStroke && layer.id === activeLayerId) {
      drawVectorStroke(targetCtx, liveStroke);
    }
    targetCtx.restore();
  };

  // Render layers in bottom-to-top order, supporting clipping masks
  for (let i = 0; i < scene.layers.length; i++) {
    const layer = scene.layers[i];
    if (!layer.visible) continue;

    // Check if subsequent layers clip to this base layer
    const clippedChildren: Layer[] = [];
    let j = i + 1;
    while (j < scene.layers.length && scene.layers[j].clippingMask) {
      if (scene.layers[j].visible) clippedChildren.push(scene.layers[j]);
      j++;
    }

    if (clippedChildren.length > 0) {
      const maskCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      maskCanvas.width = width;
      maskCanvas.height = height;
      const mctx = maskCanvas.getContext('2d');
      if (mctx) {
        renderLayerWithContent(mctx, layer);
        for (const child of clippedChildren) {
          mctx.save();
          mctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-atop';
          renderLayerWithContent(mctx, child);
          mctx.restore();
        }
        ctx.drawImage(maskCanvas, 0, 0);
      }
      i = j - 1;
    } else {
      renderLayerWithContent(ctx, layer);
    }
  }

  // Reference overlays (only in editor mode)
  if (showGuides) {
    for (const ref of scene.references) {
      if (!ref.visible) continue;
      const img = getCachedImage(ref.dataUrl, onImageLoad);
      if (img) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = ref.opacity;
        ctx.drawImage(img, ref.x, ref.y, ref.width, ref.height);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }
  }

  if (applyEffects) {
    applyCompositingEffects(ctx, width, height, scene.effects, frame);
  }

  // Guides (Grid, Perspective, Safe Area, Symmetry)
  if (showGuides && guides) {
    ctx.save();
    if (guides.grid) {
      const step = Math.max(16, guides.gridSize);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(156, 163, 175, 0.14)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let x = step; x < width; x += step) {
        ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(x, height);
      }
      for (let y = step; y < height; y += step) {
        ctx.moveTo(0, y);
        ctx.lineTo(width, y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
    }

    if (guides.perspective) {
      const horizonY = height * 0.56;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(100, 214, 197, 0.28)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.setLineDash([6, 6]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, horizonY);
      ctx.lineTo(width, horizonY);
      for (let k = -4; k <= 4; k++) {
        ctx.moveTo(width * 0.15, horizonY);
        ctx.lineTo(width, horizonY + k * 140);
        ctx.moveTo(width * 0.85, horizonY);
        ctx.lineTo(0, horizonY + k * 140);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }

    if (guides.safeArea) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(139, 124, 255, 0.35)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.25;
      ctx.setLineDash([8, 6]);
      ctx.strokeRect(width * 0.05, height * 0.05, width * 0.9, height * 0.9);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(100, 214, 197, 0.3)';
      ctx.strokeRect(width * 0.1, height * 0.1, width * 0.8, height * 0.8);
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }

    if (guides.symmetry !== 'none') {
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(139, 124, 255, 0.5)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
      ctx.setLineDash([5, 5]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      if (guides.symmetry === 'vertical' || guides.symmetry === 'quad') {
        ctx.moveTo(width / 2, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(width / 2, height);
      }
      if (guides.symmetry === 'horizontal' || guides.symmetry === 'quad') {
        ctx.moveTo(0, height / 2);
        ctx.lineTo(width, height / 2);
      }
      if (guides.symmetry === 'radial') {
        const count = Math.max(3, guides.radialSegments);
        const r = Math.max(width, height);
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          const a = (i * Math.PI * 2) / count;
          ctx.moveTo(width / 2, height / 2);
          ctx.lineTo(width / 2 + Math.cos(a) * r, height / 2 + Math.sin(a) * r);
        }
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  // 2D Rig Skeleton Overlay
  if (showRig && scene.rig.bones.length > 0) {
    const world = solveForwardKinematics(scene.rig.bones);
    ctx.save();
    for (const bone of scene.rig.bones) {
      const wb = world[bone.id];
      if (!wb) continue;
      const isSelected = bone.id === selectedBoneId;

      ctx.strokeStyle = isSelected ? '#FFFFFF' : bone.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = isSelected ? 4 : 2.5;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(wb.startX, wb.startY);
      ctx.lineTo(wb.endX, wb.endY);
      ctx.stroke();

      // Bone diamond outline
      const angle = Math.atan2(wb.endY - wb.startY, wb.endX - wb.startX);
      const perp = angle + Math.PI / 2;
      const midX = wb.startX + (wb.endX - wb.startX) * 0.25;
      const midY = wb.startY + (wb.endY - wb.startY) * 0.25;
      const w = 6;
      ctx.fillStyle = isSelected ? 'rgba(139, 124, 255, 0.45)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(wb.startX, wb.startY);
      ctx.lineTo(midX + Math.cos(perp) * w, midY + Math.sin(perp) * w);
      ctx.lineTo(wb.endX, wb.endY);
      ctx.lineTo(midX - Math.cos(perp) * w, midY - Math.sin(perp) * w);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.stroke();

      // Joint pivot circle
      ctx.fillStyle = isSelected ? '#8B7CFF' : '#17191D';
      ctx.strokeStyle = bone.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(wb.startX, wb.startY, 5.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

export function generateCelThumbnail(cel: CelFrame, sceneWidth: number, sceneHeight: number): string {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return '';
  const thumbW = 96;
  const thumbH = 54;
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = thumbW;
  c.height = thumbH;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return '';
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1B1E26';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, thumbW, thumbH);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.scale(thumbW / sceneWidth, thumbH / sceneHeight);
  drawCelContent(ctx, cel);
  ctx.restore();
  return c.toDataURL('image/png');
}

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/canvas/DrawingCanvas.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Eye,
  FlipHorizontal2,
  Hand,
  Magnet,
  Maximize2,
  Minimize2,
  RotateCcw,
  RotateCw,
  Trash2,
  ZoomIn,
  ZoomOut,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { getSymmetricPoints, solveForwardKinematics, stabilizePoints } from '../engine/math';
import { resolveCelAtFrame } from '../engine/timeline';
import type {
  BrushPreset,
  GuideConfig,
  Layer,
  OnionSkinConfig,
  Scene,
  StrokePoint,
  ToolId,
  VectorStroke,
} from '../engine/types';
import { drawCelContent, getCachedImage, renderSceneToCanvas } from './renderer';

interface DrawingCanvasProps {
  scene: Scene;
  currentFrame: number;
  activeLayer: Layer | undefined;
  activeTool: ToolId;
  activeColor: string;
  brushPreset: BrushPreset;
  shapeFill: boolean;
  onionSkin: OnionSkinConfig;
  guides: GuideConfig;
  cameraPreviewMode: boolean;
  showRigOverlay: boolean;
  rigEditMode: boolean;
  selectedBoneId: string | null;
  palmRejection: boolean;
  zoom: number;
  pan: { x: number; y: number };
  rotationDeg: number;
  onZoomChange: (z: number) => void;
  onPanChange: (p: { x: number; y: number }) => void;
  onRotationChange: (deg: number) => void;
  onToggleShapeFill: () => void;
  onCommitStrokes: (strokes: VectorStroke[]) => void;
  onUpdateCelRaster: (rasterDataUrl: string) => void;
  onReplaceCelStrokes: (strokes: VectorStroke[]) => void;
  onCommitTransformStrokes: (strokes: VectorStroke[]) => void;
  onTransformStart: () => void;
  onTransformEnd: () => void;
  onClearActiveCel: () => void;
  onPickColor: (hex: string) => void;
  onSelectBone: (boneId: string | null) => void;
  onUpdateBoneAngle: (boneId: string, angle: number, rootPos?: { x: number; y: number }) => void;
  onToggleCameraPreview: () => void;
}

function hexToRgba(hex: string): [number, number, number, number] {
  const clean = hex.replace('#', '');
  const full =
    clean.length === 3
      ? clean
          .split('')
          .map((c) => c + c)
          .join('')
      : clean.padEnd(6, '0');
  const n = parseInt(full, 16);
  return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255, 255];
}

function rgbToHex(r: number, g: number, b: number): string {
  return (
    '#' +
    [r, g, b]
      .map((v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, v)).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
      .join('')
  );
}

function pointInPolygon(x: number, y: number, poly: StrokePoint[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const xi = poly[i].x;
    const yi = poly[i].y;
    const xj = poly[j].x;
    const yj = poly[j].y;
    const intersect =
      yi > y !== yj > y && x < ((xj - xi) * (y - yi)) / (yj - yi + 1e-9) + xi;
    if (intersect) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

function floodFillCanvas(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  startX: number,
  startY: number,
  fillHex: string,
  tolerance = 36
): boolean {
  const sx = Math.floor(startX);
  const sy = Math.floor(startY);
  if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= width || sy >= height) return false;

  const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, width, height);
  const data = imgData.data;
  const idx0 = (sy * width + sx) * 4;
  const targetR = data[idx0];
  const targetG = data[idx0 + 1];
  const targetB = data[idx0 + 2];
  const targetA = data[idx0 + 3];

  const [fillR, fillG, fillB, fillA] = hexToRgba(fillHex);
  if (
    Math.abs(targetR - fillR) < 4 &&
    Math.abs(targetG - fillG) < 4 &&
    Math.abs(targetB - fillB) < 4 &&
    Math.abs(targetA - fillA) < 4
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const matches = (pos: number) => {
    return (
      Math.abs(data[pos] - targetR) <= tolerance &&
      Math.abs(data[pos + 1] - targetG) <= tolerance &&
      Math.abs(data[pos + 2] - targetB) <= tolerance &&
      Math.abs(data[pos + 3] - targetA) <= tolerance
    );
  };

  const visited = new Uint8Array(width * height);
  const stack: number[] = [sx, sy];

  while (stack.length > 0) {
    const y = stack.pop()!;
    const x = stack.pop()!;
    const pIdx = y * width + x;
    if (visited[pIdx]) continue;
    const off = pIdx * 4;
    if (!matches(off)) continue;

    visited[pIdx] = 1;
    data[off] = fillR;
    data[off + 1] = fillG;
    data[off + 2] = fillB;
    data[off + 3] = fillA;

    if (x > 0) stack.push(x - 1, y);
    if (x < width - 1) stack.push(x + 1, y);
    if (y > 0) stack.push(x, y - 1);
    if (y < height - 1) stack.push(x, y + 1);
  }

  ctx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
  return true;
}

export const DrawingCanvas: React.FC<DrawingCanvasProps> = ({
  scene,
  currentFrame,
  activeLayer,
  activeTool,
  activeColor,
  brushPreset,
  shapeFill,
  onionSkin,
  guides,
  cameraPreviewMode,
  showRigOverlay,
  rigEditMode,
  selectedBoneId,
  palmRejection,
  zoom,
  pan,
  rotationDeg,
  onZoomChange,
  onPanChange,
  onRotationChange,
  onToggleShapeFill,
  onCommitStrokes,
  onUpdateCelRaster,
  onReplaceCelStrokes,
  onCommitTransformStrokes,
  onTransformStart,
  onTransformEnd,
  onClearActiveCel,
  onPickColor,
  onSelectBone,
  onUpdateBoneAngle,
  onToggleCameraPreview,
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const activePointsRef = useRef<StrokePoint[]>([]);
  const isDrawingRef = useRef(false);
  const hasPenInputRef = useRef(false);
  const panStartRef = useRef<{ clientX: number; clientY: number; panX: number; panY: number } | null>(null);
  const transformDragRef = useRef<{
    lastX: number;
    lastY: number;
    totalDx: number;
    totalDy: number;
    moved: boolean;
    hasRaster: boolean;
    rasterDataUrl?: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const boneDragRef = useRef<{ boneId: string; startX: number; startY: number } | null>(null);
  const activePointersRef = useRef<Map<number, { x: number; y: number }>>(new Map());
  const pinchRef = useRef<{ dist: number; zoom: number } | null>(null);

  const [liveStroke, setLiveStroke] = useState<VectorStroke | null>(null);
  const [lassoPoints, setLassoPoints] = useState<StrokePoint[]>([]);
  const [selectedStrokeIds, setSelectedStrokeIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [zoomOutMode, setZoomOutMode] = useState(false);
  const [textInputPos, setTextInputPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [textDraft, setTextDraft] = useState('');
  const [renderTick, setRenderTick] = useState(0);

  // Accurate screen -> canvas projection accounting for CSS pan, zoom, and canvas rotation
  const screenToCanvas = useCallback(
    (clientX: number, clientY: number): StrokePoint => {
      const canvas = canvasRef.current;
      if (!canvas) return { x: 0, y: 0, pressure: 0.65 };
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cx = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
      const cy = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
      const dx = clientX - cx;
      const dy = clientY - cy;

      const rad = (-rotationDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
      const cos = Math.cos(rad);
      const sin = Math.sin(rad);
      const ux = dx * cos - dy * sin;
      const uy = dx * sin + dy * cos;

      const cssWidth = Math.max(1, canvas.offsetWidth * zoom);
      const cssHeight = Math.max(1, canvas.offsetHeight * zoom);
      const x = (ux / cssWidth + 0.5) * scene.width;
      const y = (uy / cssHeight + 0.5) * scene.height;
      return { x, y, pressure: 0.65 };
    },
    [scene.width, scene.height, rotationDeg, zoom]
  );

  // Clear stale selection if switching layers or frames
  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedStrokeIds([]);
  }, [activeLayer?.id, currentFrame]);

  // Main render pass
  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    renderSceneToCanvas(ctx, scene, {
      frame: currentFrame,
      activeLayerId: activeLayer?.id,
      onionSkin: cameraPreviewMode ? undefined : onionSkin,
      guides,
      showRig: showRigOverlay && !cameraPreviewMode,
      selectedBoneId,
      applyCamera: cameraPreviewMode || scene.cameraKeyframes.length > 0,
      applyEffects: true,
      showGuides: !cameraPreviewMode,
      liveStroke,
      onImageLoad: () => setRenderTick((t) => t + 1),
    });

    // Draw lasso / selection highlight overlay
    if (!cameraPreviewMode && (lassoPoints.length > 1 || selectedStrokeIds.length > 0)) {
      ctx.save();
      if (lassoPoints.length > 1) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#64D6C5';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        ctx.setLineDash([6, 4]);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(lassoPoints[0].x, lassoPoints[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < lassoPoints.length; i++) {
          ctx.lineTo(lassoPoints[i].x, lassoPoints[i].y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      if (selectedStrokeIds.length > 0 && activeLayer) {
        const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame);
        if (cel) {
          let minX = Infinity,
            minY = Infinity,
            maxX = -Infinity,
            maxY = -Infinity;
          for (const st of cel.strokes) {
            if (!selectedStrokeIds.includes(st.id)) continue;
            for (const p of st.points) {
              minX = Math.min(minX, p.x);
              minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
              maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x);
              maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y);
            }
          }
          if (isFinite(minX)) {
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#8B7CFF';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.setLineDash([5, 4]);
            ctx.strokeRect(minX - 10, minY - 10, maxX - minX + 20, maxY - minY + 20);
          }
        }
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }, [
    scene,
    currentFrame,
    activeLayer,
    onionSkin,
    guides,
    cameraPreviewMode,
    showRigOverlay,
    selectedBoneId,
    liveStroke,
    lassoPoints,
    selectedStrokeIds,
    renderTick,
  ]);

  const handleWheel = (e: React.WheelEvent) => {
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const delta = -e.deltaY * 0.002;
      onZoomChange(Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, Number((zoom + delta).toFixed(2)))));
    } else {
      onPanChange({
        x: pan.x - e.deltaX * 0.7,
        y: pan.y - e.deltaY * 0.7,
      });
    }
  };

  const applyTransformToCel = (
    transformType: 'scale-up' | 'scale-down' | 'rot-ccw' | 'rot-cw' | 'flip-h' | 'delete'
  ) => {
    if (!activeLayer || activeLayer.locked) return;
    const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame);
    if (!cel) return;

    if (cel.strokes.length === 0) {
      if (transformType === 'delete') onClearActiveCel();
      return;
    }

    const targetIds =
      selectedStrokeIds.length > 0
        ? new Set(selectedStrokeIds)
        : new Set(cel.strokes.map((s) => s.id));

    if (transformType === 'delete') {
      const remaining = cel.strokes.filter((s) => !targetIds.has(s.id));
      onCommitTransformStrokes(remaining);
      setSelectedStrokeIds([]);
      return;
    }

    // Compute bounding center of target strokes
    let minX = Infinity,
      minY = Infinity,
      maxX = -Infinity,
      maxY = -Infinity;
    for (const s of cel.strokes) {
      if (!targetIds.has(s.id)) continue;
      for (const p of s.points) {
        minX = Math.min(minX, p.x);
        minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
        maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x);
        maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y);
      }
    }
    if (!isFinite(minX)) return;
    const cx = (minX + maxX) / 2;
    const cy = (minY + maxY) / 2;

    const nextStrokes = cel.strokes.map((s) => {
      if (!targetIds.has(s.id)) return s;
      const pts = s.points.map((p) => {
        const dx = p.x - cx;
        const dy = p.y - cy;
        if (transformType === 'scale-up') {
          return { ...p, x: cx + dx * 1.12, y: cy + dy * 1.12 };
        }
        if (transformType === 'scale-down') {
          return { ...p, x: cx + dx * 0.89, y: cy + dy * 0.89 };
        }
        if (transformType === 'flip-h') {
          return { ...p, x: cx - dx };
        }
        const angleRad = ((transformType === 'rot-cw' ? 15 : -15) * Math.PI) / 180;
        const cos = Math.cos(angleRad);
        const sin = Math.sin(angleRad);
        return {
          ...p,
          x: cx + dx * cos - dy * sin,
          y: cy + dx * sin + dy * cos,
        };
      });
      return { ...s, points: pts };
    });

    onCommitTransformStrokes(nextStrokes);
  };

  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    if (e.pointerType === 'pen') {
      hasPenInputRef.current = true;
    }

    // Only reject stray single-finger palm touches if a stylus/pen has been detected
    if (palmRejection && hasPenInputRef.current && e.pointerType === 'touch' && !rigEditMode) {
      activePointersRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
      if (activePointersRef.current.size < 2) return;
    }

    activePointersRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
    if (activePointersRef.current.size === 2) {
      const pts = Array.from(activePointersRef.current.values());
      const dist = Math.hypot(pts[0].x - pts[1].x, pts[0].y - pts[1].y);
      pinchRef.current = { dist, zoom };
      isDrawingRef.current = false;
      setLiveStroke(null);
      return;
    }

    try {
      (e.currentTarget as HTMLCanvasElement).setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    } catch {
      // Ignore if pointerId cannot be captured (e.g., synthetic or transient pointer)
    }
    const pt = screenToCanvas(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    const rawPressure = e.pointerType === 'pen' && e.pressure > 0 ? e.pressure : 0.72;
    pt.pressure = brushPreset.pressureSize ? rawPressure : 0.765;

    // Rig bone dragging priority when rig edit mode is active
    if (rigEditMode && showRigOverlay && scene.rig.bones.length > 0) {
      const world = solveForwardKinematics(scene.rig.bones);
      let nearestId: string | null = null;
      let nearestDist = 30;
      for (const bone of scene.rig.bones) {
        const wb = world[bone.id];
        if (!wb) continue;
        const dEnd = Math.hypot(wb.endX - pt.x, wb.endY - pt.y);
        const dStart = Math.hypot(wb.startX - pt.x, wb.startY - pt.y);
        const d = Math.min(dEnd, dStart);
        if (d < nearestDist) {
          nearestDist = d;
          nearestId = bone.id;
        }
      }
      if (nearestId) {
        onSelectBone(nearestId);
        boneDragRef.current = { boneId: nearestId, startX: pt.x, startY: pt.y };
        return;
      }
    }

    if (activeTool === 'hand' || e.button === 1) {
      panStartRef.current = {
        clientX: e.clientX,
        clientY: e.clientY,
        panX: pan.x,
        panY: pan.y,
      };
      return;
    }

    if (activeTool === 'zoom') {
      const zoomOut = zoomOutMode || e.altKey;
      const factor = zoomOut ? -0.25 : 0.25;
      onZoomChange(Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, Number((zoom + factor).toFixed(2)))));
      return;
    }

    if (activeTool === 'eyedropper') {
      const canvas = canvasRef.current;
      const ctx = canvas?.getContext('2d');
      if (ctx) {
        const px = Math.max(0, Math.min(scene.width - 1, Math.floor(pt.x)));
        const py = Math.max(0, Math.min(scene.height - 1, Math.floor(pt.y)));
        const d = ctx.getImageData(px, py, 1, 1).data;
        onPickColor(rgbToHex(d[0], d[1], d[2]));
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!activeLayer || activeLayer.locked) return;

    if (activeTool === 'text') {
      setTextInputPos({ x: pt.x, y: pt.y });
      setTextDraft('');
      return;
    }

    if (activeTool === 'fill') {
      const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame);
      const off = document.createElement('canvas');
      off.width = scene.width;
      off.height = scene.height;
      const octx = off.getContext('2d');
      if (!octx) return;
      if (cel) {
        drawCelContent(octx, cel);
      }
      const changed = floodFillCanvas(octx, scene.width, scene.height, pt.x, pt.y, activeColor);
      if (changed) {
        onUpdateCelRaster(off.toDataURL('image/png'));
      }
      return;
    }

    if (activeTool === 'lasso') {
      isDrawingRef.current = true;
      setLassoPoints([pt]);
      return;
    }

    if (activeTool === 'transform') {
      const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame);
      onTransformStart();
      transformDragRef.current = {
        lastX: pt.x,
        lastY: pt.y,
        totalDx: 0,
        totalDy: 0,
        moved: false,
        hasRaster: Boolean(cel?.rasterDataUrl && cel.strokes.length === 0),
        rasterDataUrl: cel?.rasterDataUrl,
      };
      return;
    }

    // Drawing or Shape tools
    isDrawingRef.current = true;
    activePointsRef.current = [pt];
    const strokeTool =
      activeTool === 'pencil' ||
      activeTool === 'brush' ||
      activeTool === 'pen' ||
      activeTool === 'eraser' ||
      activeTool === 'line' ||
      activeTool === 'rect' ||
      activeTool === 'ellipse'
        ? activeTool
        : 'pen';

    const effectiveOpacity = brushPreset.pressureOpacity
      ? Math.max(0.15, brushPreset.opacity * (0.65 + pt.pressure * 0.4))
      : brushPreset.opacity;

    setLiveStroke({
      id: `live-${Date.now()}`,
      points: [pt],
      color: activeColor,
      size: brushPreset.size,
      opacity: Math.min(1, effectiveOpacity),
      hardness: brushPreset.hardness,
      texture: brushPreset.texture,
      tool: strokeTool,
      fill: strokeTool === 'rect' || strokeTool === 'ellipse' ? shapeFill : undefined,
    });
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    if (activePointersRef.current.has(e.pointerId)) {
      activePointersRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
    }
    if (activePointersRef.current.size === 2 && pinchRef.current) {
      const pts = Array.from(activePointersRef.current.values());
      const dist = Math.hypot(pts[0].x - pts[1].x, pts[0].y - pts[1].y);
      const ratio = dist / Math.max(1, pinchRef.current.dist);
      onZoomChange(Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, Number((pinchRef.current.zoom * ratio).toFixed(2)))));
      return;
    }

    const pt = screenToCanvas(e.clientX, e.clientY);

    if (boneDragRef.current) {
      const bone = scene.rig.bones.find((b) => b.id === boneDragRef.current!.boneId);
      if (bone) {
        const world = solveForwardKinematics(scene.rig.bones);
        const wb = world[bone.id];
        if (wb) {
          if (!bone.parentId && Math.hypot(pt.x - wb.startX, pt.y - wb.startY) < 20) {
            onUpdateBoneAngle(bone.id, bone.angle, { x: pt.x, y: pt.y });
          } else {
            const parentAngle =
              bone.parentId && world[bone.parentId] ? world[bone.parentId].worldAngle : 0;
            const targetWorldDeg =
              (Math.atan2(pt.y - wb.startY, pt.x - wb.startX) * 180) / Math.PI;
            onUpdateBoneAngle(bone.id, targetWorldDeg - parentAngle);
          }
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    if (panStartRef.current) {
      onPanChange({
        x: panStartRef.current.panX + (e.clientX - panStartRef.current.clientX),
        y: panStartRef.current.panY + (e.clientY - panStartRef.current.clientY),
      });
      return;
    }

    if (transformDragRef.current && activeLayer) {
      const dx = pt.x - transformDragRef.current.lastX;
      const dy = pt.y - transformDragRef.current.lastY;
      transformDragRef.current.lastX = pt.x;
      transformDragRef.current.lastY = pt.y;
      transformDragRef.current.totalDx += dx;
      transformDragRef.current.totalDy += dy;
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 0.05 || Math.abs(dy) > 0.05) {
        transformDragRef.current.moved = true;
      }

      const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame);
      if (cel && cel.strokes.length > 0) {
        const targetIds =
          selectedStrokeIds.length > 0
            ? new Set(selectedStrokeIds)
            : new Set(cel.strokes.map((s) => s.id));
        const moved = cel.strokes.map((s) =>
          targetIds.has(s.id)
            ? { ...s, points: s.points.map((p) => ({ ...p, x: p.x + dx, y: p.y + dy })) }
            : s
        );
        onReplaceCelStrokes(moved);
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!isDrawingRef.current) return;

    if (activeTool === 'lasso') {
      setLassoPoints((prev) => [...prev, pt]);
      return;
    }

    const rawPressure = e.pointerType === 'pen' && e.pressure > 0 ? e.pressure : 0.72;
    pt.pressure = brushPreset.pressureSize ? rawPressure : 0.765;

    if (activeTool === 'line' || activeTool === 'rect' || activeTool === 'ellipse') {
      const start = activePointsRef.current[0];
      activePointsRef.current = [start, pt];
    } else {
      activePointsRef.current.push(pt);
    }

    setLiveStroke((prev) =>
      prev
        ? {
            ...prev,
            points:
              activeTool === 'line' || activeTool === 'rect' || activeTool === 'ellipse'
                ? [...activePointsRef.current]
                : stabilizePoints(activePointsRef.current, brushPreset.stabilization),
          }
        : null
    );
  };

  const handlePointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    activePointersRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);
    if (activePointersRef.current.size < 2) {
      pinchRef.current = null;
    }
    boneDragRef.current = null;
    panStartRef.current = null;

    if (transformDragRef.current) {
      const td = transformDragRef.current;
      transformDragRef.current = null;
      if (td.moved && td.hasRaster && td.rasterDataUrl) {
        const img = getCachedImage(td.rasterDataUrl);
        if (img) {
          const off = document.createElement('canvas');
          off.width = scene.width;
          off.height = scene.height;
          const octx = off.getContext('2d');
          if (octx) {
            octx.drawImage(img, td.totalDx, td.totalDy);
            onUpdateCelRaster(off.toDataURL('image/png'));
          }
        }
      }
      if (td.moved) {
        onTransformEnd();
      }
    }

    if (!isDrawingRef.current) return;
    isDrawingRef.current = false;

    if (activeTool === 'lasso') {
      if (activeLayer && lassoPoints.length > 2) {
        const cel = resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame);
        if (cel) {
          const matched = cel.strokes
            .filter((s) => s.points.some((p) => pointInPolygon(p.x, p.y, lassoPoints)))
            .map((s) => s.id);
          setSelectedStrokeIds(matched);
        }
      }
      setLassoPoints([]);
      return;
    }

    if (liveStroke && liveStroke.points.length > 0) {
      const stabilized =
        liveStroke.tool === 'line' || liveStroke.tool === 'rect' || liveStroke.tool === 'ellipse'
          ? liveStroke.points
          : stabilizePoints(activePointsRef.current, brushPreset.stabilization);

      // Apply symmetry mirroring cleanly
      if (guides.symmetry !== 'none') {
        const branches: StrokePoint[][] = [];
        for (const p of stabilized) {
          const sym = getSymmetricPoints(
            p,
            scene.width,
            scene.height,
            guides.symmetry,
            guides.radialSegments
          );
          sym.forEach((sp, idx) => {
            if (!branches[idx]) branches[idx] = [];
            branches[idx].push(sp);
          });
        }
        const committed: VectorStroke[] = branches.map((pts, idx) => ({
          ...liveStroke,
          id: `st-${Date.now()}-${idx}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
          points: pts,
        }));
        onCommitStrokes(committed);
      } else {
        onCommitStrokes([
          {
            ...liveStroke,
            id: `st-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
            points: stabilized,
          },
        ]);
      }
    }

    activePointsRef.current = [];
    setLiveStroke(null);
  };

  const commitText = () => {
    if (textInputPos && textDraft.trim()) {
      onCommitStrokes([
        {
          id: `txt-${Date.now()}`,
          tool: 'text',
          color: activeColor,
          size: Math.max(6, brushPreset.size),
          opacity: brushPreset.opacity,
          hardness: 1,
          texture: 'smooth',
          points: [{ x: textInputPos.x, y: textInputPos.y, pressure: 1 }],
          text: textDraft.trim(),
        },
      ]);
    }
    setTextInputPos(null);
    setTextDraft('');
  };

  const cursorClass =
    activeTool === 'hand'
      ? 'cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing'
      : activeTool === 'zoom'
      ? zoomOutMode
        ? 'cursor-zoom-out'
        : 'cursor-zoom-in'
      : activeTool === 'transform'
      ? 'cursor-move'
      : 'cursor-crosshair';

  const showTransformBar =
    activeTool === 'transform' ||
    activeTool === 'lasso' ||
    selectedStrokeIds.length > 0;

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      onWheel={handleWheel}
      className="relative flex-1 h-full overflow-hidden bg-[#121418] select-none flex items-center justify-center"
      style={{ backgroundColor: 'var(--studio-bg)' }}
    >
      {/* Floating Contextual Bar (Transform / Shape Fill / Zoom Mode) */}
      {(showTransformBar || activeTool === 'rect' || activeTool === 'ellipse' || activeTool === 'zoom') && (
        <div className="absolute top-3 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-10 flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-[var(--studio-panel)]/95 border border-[var(--studio-border)] shadow-lg text-[11px] backdrop-blur-sm max-w-[92%] overflow-x-auto">
          {(activeTool === 'rect' || activeTool === 'ellipse') && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onToggleShapeFill}
              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-medium whitespace-nowrap ${
                shapeFill
                  ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white'
                  : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]'
              }`}
            >
              {shapeFill ? 'Shape: Filled' : 'Shape: Outline'}
            </button>
          )}

          {activeTool === 'zoom' && (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setZoomOutMode(false)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-medium ${
                  !zoomOutMode ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white' : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]'
                }`}
              >
                <ZoomIn className="w-3 h-3" /> Zoom In
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setZoomOutMode(true)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-medium ${
                  zoomOutMode ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white' : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]'
                }`}
              >
                <ZoomOut className="w-3 h-3" /> Zoom Out
              </button>
            </>
          )}

          {showTransformBar && (
            <>
              <span className="text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] px-1 whitespace-nowrap">
                {selectedStrokeIds.length > 0
                  ? `${selectedStrokeIds.length} sel`
                  : 'Cel'}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => applyTransformToCel('scale-up')}
                title="Scale Up +12%"
                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/50"
              >
                <Maximize2 className="w-3 h-3" /> +
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => applyTransformToCel('scale-down')}
                title="Scale Down -11%"
                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/50"
              >
                <Minimize2 className="w-3 h-3" /> -
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => applyTransformToCel('rot-ccw')}
                title="Rotate -15°"
                className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/50"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => applyTransformToCel('rot-cw')}
                title="Rotate +15°"
                className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/50"
              >
                <RotateCw className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => applyTransformToCel('flip-h')}
                title="Flip Horizontal"
                className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/50"
              >
                <FlipHorizontal2 className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => applyTransformToCel('delete')}
                title={selectedStrokeIds.length > 0 ? 'Delete Selected' : 'Clear Cel'}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-rose-400 hover:bg-rose-500/15 whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                {selectedStrokeIds.length > 0 ? 'Delete' : 'Clear'}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Transformed Canvas Stage */}
      <div
        className="relative shadow-2xl border border-[#343943]/80 transition-transform duration-75"
        style={{
          transform: `translate(${pan.x}px, ${pan.y}px) scale(${zoom}) rotate(${rotationDeg}deg)`,
          transformOrigin: 'center center',
        }}
      >
        <div
          className={`relative ${scene.transparentBg ? 'canvas-checkerboard' : ''}`}
          style={{
            width: 'min(72vw, 920px)',
            aspectRatio: `${scene.width} / ${scene.height}`,
          }}
        >
          <canvas
            ref={canvasRef}
            data-testid="animation-canvas"
            width={scene.width}
            height={scene.height}
            onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
            onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
            onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}
            onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}
            className={`w-full h-full block touch-none ${cursorClass}`}
          />

          {/* Floating Text Tool Input */}
          {textInputPos && (
            <div
              className="absolute z-20 flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#252930] border border-[#8B7CFF] rounded px-2 py-1 shadow-lg"
              style={{
                left: `${(textInputPos.x / scene.width) * 100}%`,
                top: `${(textInputPos.y / scene.height) * 100}%`,
              }}
            >
              <input
                autoFocus
                type="text"
                value={textDraft}
                onChange={(e) => setTextDraft(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') commitText();
                  if (e.key === 'Escape') setTextInputPos(null);
                }}
                placeholder="Type text..."
                className="bg-transparent text-xs text-white outline-none w-32"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={commitText}
                className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white rounded"
              >
                Add
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Compact Canvas Viewport Overlay Controls (Bottom-Left of stage, non-obtrusive) */}
      <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 z-10 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--studio-panel)]/90 border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-xs backdrop-blur-sm">
        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
          {scene.width}×{scene.height}
        </span>
        <span className="text-[var(--studio-border)]">|</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onToggleCameraPreview}
          aria-label="Toggle Camera Preview"
          className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
            cameraPreviewMode
              ? 'bg-[#64D6C5]/20 text-[#64D6C5]'
              : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]'
          }`}
        >
          <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          {cameraPreviewMode ? 'Camera View' : 'Stage View'}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onZoomChange(Math.max(0.2, Number((zoom - 0.15).toFixed(2))))}
          aria-label="Zoom Out Canvas"
          className="p-0.5 rounded text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
        >
          <ZoomOut className="w-3 h-3" />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            onZoomChange(1);
            onPanChange({ x: 0, y: 0 });
            onRotationChange(0);
          }}
          aria-label="Reset Canvas View"
          className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)] whitespace-nowrap font-mono tabular-nums"
          title="Reset Zoom, Pan & Rotation"
        >
          <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
          {Math.round(zoom * 100)}%
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onZoomChange(Math.min(4, Number((zoom + 0.15).toFixed(2))))}
          aria-label="Zoom In Canvas"
          className="p-0.5 rounded text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
        >
          <ZoomIn className="w-3 h-3" />
        </button>
        {rotationDeg !== 0 && (
          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px] text-[#8B7CFF]">
            {rotationDeg}°
          </span>
        )}
        {activeLayer?.locked && (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-amber-400">
            <Hand className="w-3 h-3" /> Locked
          </span>
        )}
        {selectedStrokeIds.length > 0 && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedStrokeIds([])}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-[#8B7CFF] hover:underline"
          >
            <Magnet className="w-3 h-3" /> Clear Sel ({selectedStrokeIds.length})
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/components/TopToolbar.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React from 'react';
import {
  Download,
  FolderOpen,
  HelpCircle,
  Layers,
  Moon,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Redo2,
  Repeat,
  Save,
  SkipBack,
  SkipForward,
  StepBack,
  StepForward,
  Sun,
  Undo2,
  ZoomIn,
  ZoomOut,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { StudioMode, ThemeMode } from '../engine/types';

interface TopToolbarProps {
  projectName: string;
  sceneName: string;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  loopPlayback: boolean;
  currentFrame: number;
  totalFrames: number;
  fps: 12 | 24 | 25 | 30;
  playbackSpeed: number;
  zoom: number;
  canUndo: boolean;
  canRedo: boolean;
  studioMode: StudioMode;
  theme: ThemeMode;
  lastSavedLabel: string;
  onOpenProjects: () => void;
  onSaveProject: () => void;
  onUndo: () => void;
  onRedo: () => void;
  onTogglePlay: () => void;
  onToggleLoop: () => void;
  onSeekFrame: (frame: number) => void;
  onChangeFps: (fps: 12 | 24 | 25 | 30) => void;
  onChangeSpeed: (speed: number) => void;
  onChangeZoom: (zoom: number) => void;
  onChangeMode: (mode: StudioMode) => void;
  onToggleTheme: () => void;
  onOpenShortcuts: () => void;
  onOpenExport: () => void;
  onToggleInspectorMobile: () => void;
}

export const TopToolbar: React.FC<TopToolbarProps> = ({
  projectName,
  sceneName,
  isPlaying,
  loopPlayback,
  currentFrame,
  totalFrames,
  fps,
  playbackSpeed,
  zoom,
  canUndo,
  canRedo,
  studioMode,
  theme,
  lastSavedLabel,
  onOpenProjects,
  onSaveProject,
  onUndo,
  onRedo,
  onTogglePlay,
  onToggleLoop,
  onSeekFrame,
  onChangeFps,
  onChangeSpeed,
  onChangeZoom,
  onChangeMode,
  onToggleTheme,
  onOpenShortcuts,
  onOpenExport,
  onToggleInspectorMobile,
}) => {
  return (
    <header className="h-12 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5 sm:gap-3 px-2 sm:px-3 bg-[var(--studio-surface)] border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] select-none overflow-x-auto">
      {/* Left: Brand & File Actions */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2 shrink-0">
        <span className="font-display font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-tight text-[var(--studio-text)] whitespace-nowrap pr-1.5 sm:pr-2 border-r border-[var(--studio-border)]">
          Koma Studio
        </span>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenProjects}
          aria-label="Open Projects and Scenes"
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-[var(--studio-panel)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40 border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text)] whitespace-nowrap transition-colors"
        >
          <FolderOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#8B7CFF]" />
          <span className="hidden sm:inline max-w-[130px] truncate">{projectName}</span>
        </button>

        {studioMode === 'studio' && (
          <span className="hidden xl:inline text-xs text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] whitespace-nowrap max-w-[140px] truncate">
            {sceneName}
          </span>
        )}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5 pl-0.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onUndo}
            disabled={!canUndo}
            aria-label="Undo"
            title="Undo (Ctrl+Z)"
            className="p-1.5 rounded text-[var(--studio-text)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-panel)] disabled:opacity-35 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-colors"
          >
            <Undo2 className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onRedo}
            disabled={!canRedo}
            aria-label="Redo"
            title="Redo (Ctrl+Shift+Z)"
            className="p-1.5 rounded text-[var(--studio-text)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-panel)] disabled:opacity-35 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-colors"
          >
            <Redo2 className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onSaveProject}
            aria-label="Save Project Snapshot"
            title={`Save Snapshot (${lastSavedLabel})`}
            className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-medium text-[var(--studio-text)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-panel)] whitespace-nowrap transition-colors"
          >
            <Save className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#64D6C5]" />
            <span className="hidden md:inline text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
              {lastSavedLabel}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Center: Playback, Frame Counter, FPS & Zoom */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded px-1 py-0.5 gap-0.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSeekFrame(0)}
            aria-label="First Frame"
            className="hidden sm:inline-flex p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40 text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            <SkipBack className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSeekFrame(Math.max(0, currentFrame - 1))}
            aria-label="Previous Frame"
            className="hidden sm:inline-flex p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40 text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            <StepBack className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onTogglePlay}
            aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause Animation' : 'Play Animation'}
            className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors ${
              isPlaying
                ? 'bg-[#64D6C5] text-[#111827]'
                : 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white hover:bg-[#7A6AEF]'
            }`}
          >
            {isPlaying ? <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSeekFrame((currentFrame + 1) % totalFrames)}
            aria-label="Next Frame"
            className="hidden sm:inline-flex p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40 text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            <StepForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSeekFrame(totalFrames - 1)}
            aria-label="Last Frame"
            className="hidden sm:inline-flex p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40 text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            <SkipForward className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onToggleLoop}
            aria-label="Toggle Loop Playback"
            title="Loop Playback"
            className={`hidden sm:inline-flex p-1 rounded transition-colors ${
              loopPlayback
                ? 'text-[#64D6C5] bg-[#64D6C5]/15'
                : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]'
            }`}
          >
            <Repeat className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <span
          data-testid="frame-counter"
          className="hidden xs:inline-block sm:inline-block font-mono tabular-nums text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text)] whitespace-nowrap"
        >
          {String(currentFrame + 1).padStart(3, '0')} / {String(totalFrames).padStart(3, '0')}
        </span>

        <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="fps-select">
          Frame Rate
        </label>
        <select
          id="fps-select"
          value={fps}
          onChange={(e) => onChangeFps(Number(e.target.value) as 12 | 24 | 25 | 30)}
          aria-label="Frame Rate FPS"
          className="hidden sm:block bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-[var(--studio-text)] outline-none"
        >
          <option value={12}>12 FPS</option>
          <option value={24}>24 FPS</option>
          <option value={25}>25 FPS</option>
          <option value={30}>30 FPS</option>
        </select>

        {studioMode !== 'beginner' && (
          <select
            value={playbackSpeed}
            onChange={(e) => onChangeSpeed(Number(e.target.value))}
            aria-label="Playback Speed"
            className="hidden lg:block bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded px-1.5 py-1 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-[var(--studio-text)] outline-none"
          >
            <option value={0.5}>0.5x</option>
            <option value={1}>1.0x</option>
            <option value={1.5}>1.5x</option>
            <option value={2}>2.0x</option>
          </select>
        )}

        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-0.5 bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded px-1 py-0.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChangeZoom(Math.max(0.25, Number((zoom - 0.15).toFixed(2))))}
            aria-label="Zoom Out"
            className="p-1 text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            <ZoomOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px] w-10 text-center text-[var(--studio-text)]">
            {Math.round(zoom * 100)}%
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChangeZoom(Math.min(4, Number((zoom + 0.15).toFixed(2))))}
            aria-label="Zoom In"
            className="p-1 text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            <ZoomIn className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right: Workspace Mode, Theme, Help, Export */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
        <div
          role="group"
          aria-label="Workspace Complexity Mode"
          className="hidden sm:flex items-center bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded p-0.5"
        >
          {(['beginner', 'professional', 'studio'] as StudioMode[]).map((mode) => (
            <button
              key={mode}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onChangeMode(mode)}
              className={`px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium rounded capitalize whitespace-nowrap transition-colors ${
                studioMode === mode
                  ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white'
                  : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]'
              }`}
            >
              {mode}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onToggleInspectorMobile}
          aria-label="Toggle Right Inspector"
          className="lg:hidden p-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text)]"
        >
          <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onToggleTheme}
          aria-label={`Switch to ${theme === 'dark' ? 'Light' : 'Dark'} Theme`}
          title="Toggle Dark / Light Theme"
          className="p-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)] transition-colors"
        >
          {theme === 'dark' ? <Sun className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Moon className="w-4 h-4" />}
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenShortcuts}
          aria-label="Keyboard Shortcuts and Guide"
          title="Keyboard Shortcuts & Guide"
          className="hidden sm:inline-flex p-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)] transition-colors"
        >
          <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenExport}
          aria-label="Export Animation"
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 sm:px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-semibold bg-[#8B7CFF] hover:bg-[#7A6AEF] text-white whitespace-nowrap transition-colors"
        >
          <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">Export</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/components/LeftToolRail.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Brush,
  Circle,
  Eraser,
  Hand,
  Lasso,
  Minus,
  Move,
  PaintBucket,
  PenTool,
  Pencil,
  Pipette,
  Search,
  Square,
  Type as TypeIcon,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { BrushPreset, StudioMode, ToolId } from '../engine/types';

interface LeftToolRailProps {
  activeTool: ToolId;
  activeColor: string;
  brushPreset: BrushPreset;
  studioMode: StudioMode;
  onSelectTool: (tool: ToolId) => void;
  onUpdateBrush: (patch: Partial<BrushPreset>) => void;
  onOpenColorTab: () => void;
}

const ALL_TOOLS: {
  id: ToolId;
  label: string;
  shortcut: string;
  essential: boolean;
  Icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}[] = [
  { id: 'pencil', label: 'Pencil', shortcut: 'B', essential: true, Icon: Pencil },
  { id: 'brush', label: 'Brush', shortcut: 'P', essential: true, Icon: Brush },
  { id: 'pen', label: 'Ink Pen', shortcut: 'N', essential: true, Icon: PenTool },
  { id: 'eraser', label: 'Eraser', shortcut: 'E', essential: true, Icon: Eraser },
  { id: 'fill', label: 'Fill Bucket', shortcut: 'G', essential: true, Icon: PaintBucket },
  { id: 'line', label: 'Line', shortcut: 'L', essential: false, Icon: Minus },
  { id: 'rect', label: 'Rectangle', shortcut: 'R', essential: true, Icon: Square },
  { id: 'ellipse', label: 'Ellipse', shortcut: 'O', essential: false, Icon: Circle },
  { id: 'lasso', label: 'Lasso Select', shortcut: 'Q', essential: false, Icon: Lasso },
  { id: 'transform', label: 'Transform', shortcut: 'V', essential: true, Icon: Move },
  { id: 'eyedropper', label: 'Eyedropper', shortcut: 'I', essential: false, Icon: Pipette },
  { id: 'text', label: 'Text', shortcut: 'T', essential: false, Icon: TypeIcon },
  { id: 'hand', label: 'Hand Pan', shortcut: 'H', essential: true, Icon: Hand },
  { id: 'zoom', label: 'Zoom', shortcut: 'Z', essential: false, Icon: Search },
];

export const LeftToolRail: React.FC<LeftToolRailProps> = ({
  activeTool,
  activeColor,
  brushPreset,
  studioMode,
  onSelectTool,
  onUpdateBrush,
  onOpenColorTab,
}) => {
  const [showAllInBeginner, setShowAllInBeginner] = useState(false);

  const visibleTools =
    studioMode === 'beginner' && !showAllInBeginner
      ? ALL_TOOLS.filter((t) => t.essential)
      : ALL_TOOLS;

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="Drawing and Selection Tools"
      className="w-12 sm:w-[78px] shrink-0 h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-between py-2 bg-[var(--studio-surface)] border-r border-[var(--studio-border)] select-none overflow-y-auto"
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-1 w-full px-1.5">
        {visibleTools.map(({ id, label, shortcut, Icon }) => {
          const isActive = activeTool === id;
          return (
            <button
              key={id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectTool(id)}
              aria-label={`${label} (${shortcut})`}
              aria-pressed={isActive}
              title={`${label} (${shortcut})`}
              className={`w-full h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded transition-colors ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-panel)]'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          );
        })}

        {studioMode === 'beginner' && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowAllInBeginner((v) => !v)}
            aria-label={showAllInBeginner ? 'Show Fewer Tools' : 'Show More Tools'}
            title={showAllInBeginner ? 'Fewer Tools' : 'All Tools'}
            className="sm:col-span-2 w-full h-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-panel)]"
          >
            {showAllInBeginner ? (
              <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            )}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Quick Size & Active Color Swatch */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--studio-border)] w-full px-1.5 shrink-0">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center w-full gap-0.5">
          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
            {brushPreset.size}px
          </span>
          <input
            type="range"
            min={1}
            max={72}
            value={brushPreset.size}
            aria-label="Quick Brush Size"
            onChange={(e) => onUpdateBrush({ size: Number(e.target.value) })}
            className="w-9 sm:w-14 accent-[#8B7CFF] cursor-pointer"
          />
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenColorTab}
          aria-label={`Active Color ${activeColor}`}
          title={`Active Color: ${activeColor}`}
          className="w-8 h-8 rounded border-2 border-[var(--studio-border)] shadow-inner transition-transform hover:scale-105"
          style={{ backgroundColor: activeColor }}
        />
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/components/ColorWheel.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  CURATED_PALETTES,
  cmykToRgb,
  generateColorHarmonies,
  hexToRgb,
  hsvToHex,
  normalizeHex,
  rgbToCmyk,
  rgbToHex,
  rgbToHsv,
} from '../engine/color';

type ColorModeTab = 'wheel' | 'hsv' | 'rgb' | 'cmyk' | 'harmony';

interface ColorWheelProps {
  color: string;
  palette: string[];
  recentColors?: string[];
  onChangeColor: (hex: string) => void;
  onAddPaletteSwatch: (hex: string) => void;
}

export const ColorWheel: React.FC<ColorWheelProps> = ({
  color,
  palette,
  recentColors = [],
  onChangeColor,
  onAddPaletteSwatch,
}) => {
  const wheelRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ColorModeTab>('wheel');
  const [selectedPaletteId, setSelectedPaletteId] = useState<string>('project');
  const [hexDraft, setHexDraft] = useState(color);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHexDraft(color);
  }, [color]);

  const rgb = useMemo(() => hexToRgb(color), [color]);
  const hsv = useMemo(() => rgbToHsv(rgb), [rgb]);
  const cmyk = useMemo(() => rgbToCmyk(rgb), [rgb]);
  const harmonies = useMemo(() => generateColorHarmonies(color), [color]);
  const brightness = Math.max(0.15, hsv.v / 100);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode !== 'wheel') return;
    const canvas = wheelRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    const size = canvas.width;
    const r = size / 2;
    const img = ctx.createImageData(size, size);

    for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
        const dx = x - r;
        const dy = y - r;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        const off = (y * size + x) * 4;
        if (dist > r) {
          img.data[off + 3] = 0;
          continue;
        }
        const hue = ((Math.atan2(dy, dx) * 180) / Math.PI + 360) % 360;
        const sat = Math.min(1, dist / r);
        const f = (n: number, k = (n + hue / 60) % 6) =>
          brightness - brightness * sat * Math.max(Math.min(k, 4 - k, 1), 0);
        img.data[off] = Math.round(f(5) * 255);
        img.data[off + 1] = Math.round(f(3) * 255);
        img.data[off + 2] = Math.round(f(1) * 255);
        img.data[off + 3] = 255;
      }
    }
    ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  }, [brightness, mode]);

  const pickFromWheel = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    const canvas = wheelRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * canvas.width;
