                          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{Math.round(kf.rotation)}°</span>
                        </div>
                        <input
                          type="range"
                          min={-45}
                          max={45}
                          step={0.5}
                          value={kf.rotation}
                          onChange={(e) => upsertCam({ rotation: Number(e.target.value) })}
                          className="w-full accent-[#8B7CFF]"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                          <span>Shake</span>
                          <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{Math.round(kf.shake)}px</span>
                        </div>
                        <input
                          type="range"
                          min={0}
                          max={28}
                          value={kf.shake}
                          onChange={(e) => upsertCam({ shake: Number(e.target.value) })}
                          className="w-full accent-rose-400"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">Easing</span>
                      <select
                        value={kf.easing}
                        onChange={(e) => upsertCam({ easing: e.target.value as EasingType })}
                        className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[10px]"
                      >
                        <option value="linear">Linear</option>
                        <option value="ease-in">Ease In</option>
                        <option value="ease-out">Ease Out</option>
                        <option value="ease-in-out">Ease In-Out</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })()}
            </div>

            {/* Non-Destructive Anime Compositing VFX */}
            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-[var(--studio-border)] space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-display font-semibold text-xs text-[var(--studio-text)]">
                Anime Compositing & VFX
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {scene.effects.map((fx) => (
                  <div
                    key={fx.id}
                    className="p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] space-y-1.5"
                  >
                    <label className="flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-medium">{fx.label}</span>
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={fx.enabled}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          onUpdateEffects(
                            scene.effects.map((item) =>
                              item.id === fx.id ? { ...item, enabled: e.target.checked } : item
                            )
                          )
                        }
                        className="accent-[#8B7CFF]"
                      />
                    </label>
                    {fx.enabled && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] w-14">
                          Strength
                        </span>
                        <input
                          type="range"
                          min={0.05}
                          max={1}
                          step={0.05}
                          value={fx.intensity}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            onUpdateEffects(
                              scene.effects.map((item) =>
                                item.id === fx.id
                                  ? { ...item, intensity: Number(e.target.value) }
                                  : item
                              )
                            )
                          }
                          className="flex-1 accent-[#64D6C5]"
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'ai' && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <h2 className="font-display font-semibold text-xs text-[var(--studio-text)]">
                Optional AI Production Assistant
              </h2>
            </div>

            {aiStatus && !aiStatus.available && (
              <div className="p-2.5 rounded bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/40 text-amber-300 text-[11px] space-y-1">
                <p className="font-semibold">AI Backend Not Configured</p>
                <p className="opacity-90">
                  {aiStatus.message} All manual drawing, animation, rigging, and compositing tools
                  remain 100% available.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="font-medium text-[11px]">A. AI In-Betweening</span>
                  <select
                    value={inbetweenCount}
                    onChange={(e) => setInbetweenCount(Number(e.target.value))}
                    aria-label="Intermediate Frame Count"
                    className="bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px]"
                  >
                    <option value={1}>1 Frame</option>
                    <option value={2}>2 Frames</option>
                    <option value={3}>3 Frames</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={aiBusy}
                  onClick={() => runAiTask('inbetween')}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#8B7CFF] hover:bg-[#7A6AEF] text-white text-[11px] font-medium disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Wand2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  {aiBusy && aiTaskType === 'inbetween'
                    ? 'Generating Proposal...'
                    : 'Propose In-Betweens'}
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={aiBusy}
                  onClick={() => runAiTask('smart-color')}
                  className="p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[#64D6C5] text-left text-[11px]"
                >
                  <div className="font-medium">Smart Coloring</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                    Detect gaps & palette
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={aiBusy}
                  onClick={() => runAiTask('line-art')}
                  className="p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[#64D6C5] text-left text-[11px]"
                >
                  <div className="font-medium">Line Art Assist</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                    Clean-up & broken lines
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={aiBusy}
                  onClick={() => runAiTask('consistency')}
                  className="p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[#64D6C5] text-left text-[11px]"
                >
                  <div className="font-medium">Model Consistency</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                    Compare vs Ref Sheet
                  </div>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={aiBusy}
                  onClick={() => runAiTask('timing')}
                  className="p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[#64D6C5] text-left text-[11px]"
                >
                  <div className="font-medium">Timing Review</div>
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                    Spacing & X-sheet tips
                  </div>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {aiError && (
              <div className="p-2 rounded bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/40 text-rose-300 text-[11px]">
                {aiError}
              </div>
            )}

            {aiResult && (
              <div className="p-2.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[#64D6C5]/50 space-y-2 text-[11px]">
                <p className="font-medium text-[#64D6C5]">AI Proposal (Non-Destructive)</p>
                {typeof aiResult.summary === 'string' && <p>{aiResult.summary}</p>}
                {Array.isArray(aiResult.frames) && aiResult.frames.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="flex gap-1.5 pt-1">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        const proposed = (
                          aiResult.frames as {
                            strokes?: {
                              color?: string;
                              size?: number;
                              points?: { x: number; y: number }[];
                            }[];
                          }[]
                        ).map((f, fIdx) =>
                          (f.strokes ?? []).map((s, sIdx) => ({
                            id: `ai-${Date.now()}-${fIdx}-${sIdx}`,
                            tool: 'pen' as const,
                            color: s.color || activeColor,
                            size: s.size || 4,
                            opacity: 1,
                            hardness: 0.9,
                            texture: 'smooth' as const,
                            points: (s.points ?? []).map((p) => ({
                              x: p.x,
                              y: p.y,
                              pressure: 0.75,
                            })),
                          }))
                        );
                        onInsertAiInbetweenFrames(proposed);
                        setAiResult(null);
                      }}
                      className="flex-1 py-1 rounded bg-[#64D6C5] text-[#111827] font-semibold"
                    >
                      Accept & Insert
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setAiResult(null)}
                      className="px-3 py-1 rounded border border-[var(--studio-border)]"
                    >
                      Reject
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/components/BottomTimeline.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AudioWaveform,
  Camera,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Copy,
  Flag,
  Layers,
  Minus,
  Plus,
  Table2,
  Trash2,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { generateCelThumbnail } from '../canvas/renderer';
import { resolveCelAtFrame } from '../engine/timeline';
import type {
  AudioTrack,
  CelFrame,
  EasingType,
  OnionSkinConfig,
  Scene,
  StudioMode,
} from '../engine/types';

interface BottomTimelineProps {
  scene: Scene;
  currentFrame: number;
  activeLayerId: string;
  onionSkin: OnionSkinConfig;
  studioMode: StudioMode;
  onSeekFrame: (frame: number) => void;
  onSelectLayer: (layerId: string) => void;
  onUpdateOnionSkin: (patch: Partial<OnionSkinConfig>) => void;
  onInsertKeyframe: () => void;
  onDuplicateCel: () => void;
  onDeleteCel: () => void;
  onAdjustExposure: (delta: -1 | 1) => void;
  onMoveCel: (layerId: string, celId: string, targetFrame: number) => void;
  onAddTimelineMarker: (frame: number, label: string) => void;
  onAddTransformKeyframe: (easing: EasingType) => void;
  onUpdateTotalFrames: (total: number) => void;
  onAddAudioTrack: (file: File) => void;
  onUpdateAudioTrack: (id: string, patch: Partial<AudioTrack>) => void;
}

export const BottomTimeline: React.FC<BottomTimelineProps> = ({
  scene,
  currentFrame,
  activeLayerId,
  onionSkin,
  studioMode,
  onSeekFrame,
  onSelectLayer,
  onUpdateOnionSkin,
  onInsertKeyframe,
  onDuplicateCel,
  onDeleteCel,
  onAdjustExposure,
  onMoveCel,
  onAddTimelineMarker,
  onAddTransformKeyframe,
  onUpdateTotalFrames,
  onAddAudioTrack,
  onUpdateAudioTrack,
}) => {
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'timeline' | 'xsheet'>('timeline');
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [cellWidth, setCellWidth] = useState(32);
  const [dragInfo, setDragInfo] = useState<{ layerId: string; celId: string } | null>(null);
  const [easingChoice, setEasingChoice] = useState<EasingType>('ease-in-out');

  const frames = useMemo(
    () => Array.from({ length: scene.totalFrames }, (_, i) => i),
    [scene.totalFrames]
  );

  // Cache thumbnails per cel id + stroke count for crisp timeline rendering
  const thumbnails = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const layer of scene.layers) {
      for (const cel of layer.cels) {
        if (cel.strokes.length > 0 || cel.rasterDataUrl) {
          map.set(cel.id, generateCelThumbnail(cel, scene.width, scene.height));
        }
      }
    }
    return map;
  }, [scene.layers, scene.width, scene.height]);

  const activeLayer = scene.layers.find((l) => l.id === activeLayerId) ?? scene.layers[0];
  const activeCel = activeLayer ? resolveCelAtFrame(activeLayer, currentFrame) : null;

  return (
    <section
      aria-label="Animation Timeline and Exposure Sheet"
      className={`shrink-0 flex flex-col bg-[var(--studio-surface)] border-t border-[var(--studio-border)] select-none transition-all ${
        collapsed ? 'h-10' : 'h-56 sm:h-64'
      }`}
    >
      {/* Timeline Toolbar */}
      <div className="h-10 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] overflow-x-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
          {/* Timeline / X-Sheet Switch */}
          {studioMode !== 'beginner' && (
            <div className="flex items-center bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded p-0.5 mr-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setViewMode('timeline')}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium ${
                  viewMode === 'timeline'
                    ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white'
                    : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]'
                }`}
              >
                <Layers className="w-3 h-3" /> Timeline
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setViewMode('xsheet')}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-medium ${
                  viewMode === 'xsheet'
                    ? 'bg-[#64D6C5] text-[#111827]'
                    : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]'
                }`}
              >
                <Table2 className="w-3 h-3" /> X-Sheet
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onInsertKeyframe}
            aria-label="Insert New Keyframe"
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[#8B7CFF] hover:bg-[#7A6AEF] text-white text-[11px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3 h-3" /> Keyframe
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onDuplicateCel}
            aria-label="Duplicate Current Frame"
            className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[#8B7CFF] text-[11px] whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            <Copy className="w-3 h-3" /> Dup
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onAdjustExposure(-1)}
              aria-label="Decrease Exposure Duration"
              title="Hold -1 Frame"
              className="px-1.5 py-1 hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40"
            >
              <Minus className="w-3 h-3" />
            </button>
            <span className="px-1.5 font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
              {activeCel ? `${activeCel.durationFrames}f` : '0f'}
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onAdjustExposure(1)}
              aria-label="Increase Exposure Duration"
              title="Hold +1 Frame"
              className="px-1.5 py-1 hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/40"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onDeleteCel}
            aria-label="Delete Current Cel"
            className="p-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-rose-400"
          >
            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Onion Skin & Marker & Curve Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)]">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onUpdateOnionSkin({ enabled: !onionSkin.enabled })}
              aria-label="Toggle Onion Skinning"
              aria-pressed={onionSkin.enabled}
              className={`text-[11px] font-medium px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${
                onionSkin.enabled
                  ? 'bg-[#64D6C5] text-[#111827]'
                  : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]'
              }`}
            >
              Onion Skin
            </button>
            {onionSkin.enabled && (
              <>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  min={0.1}
                  max={0.75}
                  step={0.05}
                  value={onionSkin.opacity}
                  onChange={(e) => onUpdateOnionSkin({ opacity: Number(e.target.value) })}
                  aria-label="Onion Skin Opacity"
                  className="w-14 accent-[#64D6C5]"
                />
                <select
                  value={onionSkin.prevFrames}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const n = Number(e.target.value);
                    onUpdateOnionSkin({ prevFrames: n, nextFrames: n });
                  }}
                  aria-label="Onion Skin Neighbor Count"
                  className="bg-transparent text-[11px] font-mono"
                >
                  <option value={1}>±1</option>
                  <option value={2}>±2</option>
                  <option value={3}>±3</option>
                </select>
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {studioMode !== 'beginner' && (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onAddTimelineMarker(currentFrame, `M${currentFrame + 1}`)}
                aria-label="Add Timeline Marker"
                className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[11px] hover:border-[#8B7CFF]"
              >
                <Flag className="w-3 h-3 text-[#8B7CFF]" /> Marker
              </button>

              <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1">
                <select
                  value={easingChoice}
                  onChange={(e) => setEasingChoice(e.target.value as EasingType)}
                  aria-label="Transform Interpolation Curve"
                  className="bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] rounded px-1.5 py-1 text-[11px]"
                >
                  <option value="linear">Linear</option>
                  <option value="ease-in">Ease In</option>
                  <option value="ease-out">Ease Out</option>
                  <option value="ease-in-out">Ease In-Out</option>
                  <option value="bezier">Cubic Bezier</option>
                </select>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onAddTransformKeyframe(easingChoice)}
                  className="px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[11px] hover:border-[#64D6C5]"
                >
                  + Tween Key
                </button>
              </div>

              <label className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[11px] cursor-pointer hover:border-[#64D6C5]">
                <AudioWaveform className="w-3 h-3 text-[#64D6C5]" />
                <span>Audio</span>
                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept="audio/*"
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const f = e.target.files?.[0];
                    if (f) onAddAudioTrack(f);
                    e.target.value = '';
                  }}
                  className="hidden"
                />
              </label>
            </>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">Len</span>
            <input
              type="number"
              min={8}
              max={240}
              value={scene.totalFrames}
              onChange={(e) => onUpdateTotalFrames(Math.max(8, Math.min(240, Number(e.target.value))))}
              aria-label="Total Timeline Frames"
              className="w-12 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px]"
            />
          </div>

          <input
            type="range"
            min={20}
            max={56}
            value={cellWidth}
            onChange={(e) => setCellWidth(Number(e.target.value))}
            aria-label="Timeline Zoom"
            className="hidden sm:block w-14 accent-[#8B7CFF]"
          />

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setCollapsed((c) => !c)}
            aria-label={collapsed ? 'Expand Timeline' : 'Collapse Timeline'}
            className="p-1 rounded text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--studio-text)]"
          >
            {collapsed ? <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Timeline or X-Sheet Body */}
      {!collapsed && viewMode === 'timeline' && (
        <div className="flex-1 flex overflow-hidden">
          {/* Fixed Left Track Labels */}
          <div className="w-36 sm:w-44 shrink-0 border-r border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-panel)] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="h-7 px-2 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[10px] font-mono text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
              <span>TRACKS</span>
              <span>{scene.fps} FPS</span>
            </div>

            {studioMode !== 'beginner' && (
              <div className="h-7 px-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/70 text-[11px] text-[#64D6C5]">
                <Camera className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span className="truncate">Camera 2D</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {[...scene.layers].reverse().map((layer) => (
              <button
                key={layer.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectLayer(layer.id)}
                className={`w-full h-11 px-2 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/60 text-left text-[11px] ${
                  layer.id === activeLayerId
                    ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF]/15 text-[var(--studio-text)] font-medium'
                    : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-bg)]/40'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="truncate">{layer.name}</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[10px] opacity-70">{layer.cels.length}</span>
              </button>
            ))}

            {scene.audioTracks.map((track) => (
              <div
                key={track.id}
                className="h-9 px-2 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/60 text-[11px]"
              >
                <span className="truncate text-[#64D6C5]">{track.name}</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onUpdateAudioTrack(track.id, { muted: !track.muted })}
                  aria-label={`Mute ${track.name}`}
                  className="p-0.5"
                >
                  {track.muted ? (
                    <VolumeX className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />
                  ) : (
                    <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#64D6C5]" />
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Scrollable Frame Grid */}
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto relative">
            <div style={{ width: scene.totalFrames * cellWidth }}>
              {/* Frame Ruler */}
              <div className="h-7 flex border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-panel)] sticky top-0 z-10">
                {frames.map((f) => {
                  const isCurrent = f === currentFrame;
                  const marker = scene.markers.find((m) => m.frame === f);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={f}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onSeekFrame(f)}
                      style={{ width: cellWidth }}
                      className={`shrink-0 h-full border-r border-[var(--studio-border)]/50 font-mono tabular-nums text-[10px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative ${
                        isCurrent
                          ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white font-semibold'
                          : 'text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] hover:bg-[var(--studio-border)]/30'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span>{f + 1}</span>
                      {marker && (
                        <span
                          title={marker.label}
                          className="absolute top-0.5 right-0.5 w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: marker.color }}
                        />
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Camera Track Row */}
              {studioMode !== 'beginner' && (
                <div className="h-7 flex border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/70 relative bg-[var(--studio-bg)]/40">
                  {frames.map((f) => {
                    const hasCamKey = scene.cameraKeyframes.some((k) => k.frame === f);
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={f}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onSeekFrame(f)}
                        style={{ width: cellWidth }}
                        className="shrink-0 h-full border-r border-[var(--studio-border)]/30 flex items-center justify-center"
                      >
                        {hasCamKey && (
                          <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rotate-45 bg-[#64D6C5] block" />
                        )}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Cel Layer Rows */}
              {[...scene.layers].reverse().map((layer) => (
                <div
                  key={layer.id}
                  className="h-11 flex border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/60 relative"
                >
                  {frames.map((f) => {
                    const celStartingHere = layer.cels.find((c) => c.frameIndex === f);
                    const isCovered = Boolean(resolveCelAtFrame(layer, f));
                    const isCurrent = f === currentFrame;
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={f}
                        onClick={() => {
                          onSelectLayer(layer.id);
                          onSeekFrame(f);
                        }}
                        onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                        onDrop={() => {
                          if (dragInfo && dragInfo.layerId === layer.id) {
                            onMoveCel(layer.id, dragInfo.celId, f);
                            setDragInfo(null);
                          }
                        }}
                        style={{ width: cellWidth }}
                        className={`shrink-0 h-full border-r border-[var(--studio-border)]/35 relative cursor-pointer ${
                          isCurrent ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF]/12' : ''
                        } ${!celStartingHere && isCovered ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF]/8' : ''}`}
                      >
                        {celStartingHere && (
                          <div
                            draggable
                            onDragStart={() =>
                              setDragInfo({ layerId: layer.id, celId: celStartingHere.id })
                            }
                            style={{
                              width: Math.max(
                                cellWidth - 2,
                                celStartingHere.durationFrames * cellWidth - 2
                              ),
                            }}
                            title={`Drag to move cel (Frame ${f + 1}, ${celStartingHere.durationFrames}f)`}
                            className="absolute top-1 left-[1px] bottom-1 z-[5] rounded bg-[#2A2F3A] border border-[#8B7CFF]/80 overflow-hidden flex items-center px-1 gap-1 shadow-sm"
                          >
                            {thumbnails.get(celStartingHere.id) ? (
                              <img
                                src={thumbnails.get(celStartingHere.id)}
                                alt={`Cel ${f + 1}`}
                                className="h-7 w-10 object-cover rounded-sm shrink-0 pointer-events-none"
                              />
                            ) : (
                              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#8B7CFF] shrink-0" />
                            )}
                            {celStartingHere.durationFrames > 1 && (
                              <span className="font-mono text-[9px] text-[#9CA3AF] truncate">
                                {celStartingHere.durationFrames}f
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ))}

              {/* Audio Track Waveform Rows */}
              {scene.audioTracks.map((track) => (
                <div
                  key={track.id}
                  className="h-9 flex items-center border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/60 relative bg-[#64D6C5]/5 px-1"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-end gap-[2px] h-6">
                    {track.peaks.map((pk, idx) => (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className="w-1.5 rounded-t bg-[#64D6C5]/75"
                        style={{ height: `${Math.max(15, Math.round(pk * 100))}%` }}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                  {track.lipSyncMarkers.map((lm) => (
                    <span
                      key={lm.id}
                      style={{ left: lm.frame * cellWidth + 2 }}
                      className="absolute top-0.5 px-1 rounded bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white font-mono text-[9px]"
                    >
                      {lm.mouthShape}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Exposure Sheet (X-Sheet) Mode */}
      {!collapsed && viewMode === 'xsheet' && (
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
            <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-1.5 px-3 w-16">FRAME</th>
                {scene.layers.map((l) => (
                  <th key={l.id} className="py-1.5 px-3">
                    {l.name}
                  </th>
                ))}
                <th className="py-1.5 px-3">CAMERA / LIP-SYNC</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {frames.map((f) => {
                const isCurrent = f === currentFrame;
                const cam = scene.cameraKeyframes.find((k) => k.frame === f);
                const lip = scene.audioTracks
                  .flatMap((t) => t.lipSyncMarkers)
                  .find((m) => m.frame === f);
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={f}
                    onClick={() => onSeekFrame(f)}
                    className={`border-b border-[var(--studio-border)]/40 cursor-pointer ${
                      isCurrent ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF]/20' : 'hover:bg-[var(--studio-panel)]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <td className="py-1 px-3 font-mono tabular-nums font-medium">{f + 1}</td>
                    {scene.layers.map((l) => {
                      const exact = l.cels.find((c) => c.frameIndex === f);
                      const held = resolveCelAtFrame(l, f);
                      return (
                        <td key={l.id} className="py-1 px-3 font-mono text-[11px]">
                          {exact ? (
                            <span className="text-[#8B7CFF] font-semibold">
                              ● {exact.label || `Cel ${f + 1}`}
                            </span>
                          ) : held ? (
                            <span className="text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">| (Hold)</span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="opacity-25">—</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      );
                    })}
                    <td className="py-1 px-3 font-mono text-[11px] text-[#64D6C5]">
                      {cam ? `Cam(${cam.zoom.toFixed(2)}x) ` : ''}
                      {lip ? `Mouth:${lip.mouthShape}` : ''}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
};

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/components/Modals.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import JSZip from 'jszip';
import {
  Download,
  FileCode2,
  Film,
  History,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Keyboard,
  Plus,
  Sparkles,
  Upload,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { encodeTracksToWav } from '../audio/audioEngine';
import { renderSceneToCanvas } from '../canvas/renderer';
import { encodeAnimatedGif } from '../engine/gifEncoder';
import { parsePsdBuffer } from '../engine/psdImport';
import {
  listProjectVersions,
  parseProjectFile,
  serializeProjectFile,
} from '../engine/projectStore';
import { createBlankScene, createDemoScene } from '../engine/templates';
import type {
  Layer,
  ProjectVersion,
  Scene,
  ShortcutMap,
  StudioProject,
} from '../engine/types';

interface ProjectModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  project: StudioProject;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdateProject: (proj: StudioProject) => void;
  onImportLayers: (layers: Layer[]) => void;
  onSaveNamedVersion: (label: string) => Promise<void>;
}

export const ProjectModal: React.FC<ProjectModalProps> = ({
  open,
  project,
  onClose,
  onUpdateProject,
  onImportLayers,
  onSaveNamedVersion,
}) => {
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<ProjectVersion[]>([]);
  const [versionLabel, setVersionLabel] = useState('');
  const [newWidth, setNewWidth] = useState(1280);
  const [newHeight, setNewHeight] = useState(720);
  const [newFps, setNewFps] = useState<12 | 24 | 25 | 30>(24);
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      listProjectVersions().then(setVersions);
      setStatusMsg(null);
    }
  }, [open]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const handleDownloadJson = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([serializeProjectFile(project)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `${project.name.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]+/gi, '_').toLowerCase()}.koma.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleOpenProjectFile = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const txt = await file.text();
      const loaded = parseProjectFile(txt);
      onUpdateProject(loaded);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load project file');
    }
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleImportArtwork = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files ?? []);
    if (files.length === 0) return;

    try {
      const first = files[0];
      if (first.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.psd')) {
        const buf = await first.arrayBuffer();
        const psd = parsePsdBuffer(buf);
        const importedLayers: Layer[] = psd.layers.map((pl, idx) => {
          const c = document.createElement('canvas');
          c.width = psd.width;
          c.height = psd.height;
          const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
          const lc = document.createElement('canvas');
          lc.width = pl.width;
          lc.height = pl.height;
          const lctx = lc.getContext('2d')!;
          const imgData = lctx.createImageData(pl.width, pl.height);
          imgData.data.set(pl.rgba);
          lctx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
          ctx.drawImage(lc, pl.left, pl.top);

          return {
            id: `psd-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
            name: pl.name,
            type: 'cel',
            visible: pl.visible,
            locked: false,
            clippingMask: false,
            blendMode: 'normal',
            opacity: pl.opacity,
            parallaxDepth: 1,
            cels: [
              {
                id: `psd-cel-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
                frameIndex: 0,
                durationFrames: 12,
                isKey: true,
                rasterDataUrl: c.toDataURL('image/png'),
                strokes: [],
              },
            ],
            keyframes: [],
          };
        });
        if (importedLayers.length === 0) {
          setStatusMsg('PSD file contained no readable layer channels.');
          return;
        }
        onImportLayers(importedLayers);
        setStatusMsg(`Imported ${importedLayers.length} editable PSD layers.`);
      } else {
        // PNG / JPEG / SVG / Image Sequence -> single layer with sequential cels
        const cels = await Promise.all(
          files.map(
            (file, idx) =>
              new Promise<{
                id: string;
                frameIndex: number;
                durationFrames: number;
                isKey: boolean;
                rasterDataUrl: string;
                strokes: [];
              }>((resolve, reject) => {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = () =>
                  resolve({
                    id: `imp-cel-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
                    frameIndex: idx * 2,
                    durationFrames: 2,
                    isKey: true,
                    rasterDataUrl: String(reader.result),
                    strokes: [],
                  });
                reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
              })
          )
        );
        onImportLayers([
          {
            id: `imp-layer-${Date.now()}`,
            name: files.length > 1 ? 'Imported Sequence' : files[0].name,
            type: 'cel',
            visible: true,
            locked: false,
            clippingMask: false,
            blendMode: 'normal',
            opacity: 1,
            parallaxDepth: 1,
            cels,
            keyframes: [],
          },
        ]);
        setStatusMsg(`Imported ${cels.length} frame(s) into a new cel layer.`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setStatusMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Import failed');
    }
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleAddScene = () => {
    const newScene: Scene = createBlankScene(
      `scene-${Date.now()}`,
      `Cut ${String(project.scenes.length + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
      newWidth,
      newHeight,
      newFps
    );
    onUpdateProject({
      ...project,
      activeSceneId: newScene.id,
      scenes: [...project.scenes, newScene],
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/65 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="Projects, Scenes and Imports"
        className="w-full max-w-2xl max-h-[88vh] flex flex-col rounded-lg bg-[var(--studio-surface)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden text-xs"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-panel)]">
          <h2 className="font-display font-semibold text-sm text-[var(--studio-text)]">
            Project Manager, Scenes & Imports
          </h2>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close Modal" className="p-1">
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-5">
          {statusMsg && (
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded bg-[#64D6C5]/15 border border-[#64D6C5]/50 text-[#64D6C5]">
              {statusMsg}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Project Name & File Actions */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
              Project Title
            </label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={project.name}
                onChange={(e) => onUpdateProject({ ...project, name: e.target.value })}
                className="flex-1 min-w-[200px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] text-[var(--studio-text)]"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDownloadJson}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white font-medium"
              >
                <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Save .koma.json
              </button>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] cursor-pointer hover:border-[#8B7CFF]">
                <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Open Project
                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept=".json,.koma.json"
                  onChange={handleOpenProjectFile}
                  className="hidden"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Scenes / Cuts */}
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--studio-border)]">
            <h3 className="font-display font-semibold text-xs text-[var(--studio-text)]">
              Scenes / Production Cuts ({project.scenes.length})
            </h3>
            <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              {project.scenes.map((sc) => (
                <button
                  key={sc.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onUpdateProject({ ...project, activeSceneId: sc.id });
                    onClose();
                  }}
                  className={`p-2.5 rounded border text-left transition-colors ${
                    sc.id === project.activeSceneId
                      ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF]/15 border-[#8B7CFF]'
                      : 'bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[var(--studio-text-muted)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="font-medium text-[var(--studio-text)]">{sc.name}</div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-[10px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                    {sc.width}×{sc.height} · {sc.fps} FPS · {sc.totalFrames} frames
                  </div>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1">
              <select
                value={`${newWidth}x${newHeight}`}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const [w, h] = e.target.value.split('x').map(Number);
                  setNewWidth(w);
                  setNewHeight(h);
                }}
                aria-label="New Scene Resolution"
                className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono text-[11px]"
              >
                <option value="1920x1080">1920×1080 (Full HD)</option>
                <option value="1280x720">1280×720 (HD Anime)</option>
                <option value="1080x1080">1080×1080 (Square)</option>
                <option value="1080x1920">1080×1920 (Vertical Reel)</option>
              </select>
              <select
                value={newFps}
                onChange={(e) => setNewFps(Number(e.target.value) as 12 | 24 | 25 | 30)}
                aria-label="New Scene FPS"
                className="px-2 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono text-[11px]"
              >
                <option value={12}>12 FPS</option>
                <option value={24}>24 FPS</option>
                <option value={25}>25 FPS</option>
                <option value={30}>30 FPS</option>
              </select>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleAddScene}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#64D6C5] text-[#111827] font-semibold"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Add Scene
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  const demo = createDemoScene(`scene-${Date.now()}`, 'Anime Action Template');
                  onUpdateProject({
                    ...project,
                    activeSceneId: demo.id,
                    scenes: [...project.scenes, demo],
                  });
                  onClose();
                }}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] hover:border-[#8B7CFF]"
              >
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#8B7CFF]" /> Load Demo Template
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Artwork Import */}
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--studio-border)]">
            <h3 className="font-display font-semibold text-xs text-[var(--studio-text)]">
              Import Artwork & Image Sequences
            </h3>
            <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 p-3 rounded border border-dashed border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-panel)] hover:border-[#64D6C5] cursor-pointer">
              <ImageIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-[#64D6C5]" />
              <span>
                Import PNG, JPEG, SVG, Image Sequence (multi-select), or Layered PSD (.psd)
              </span>
              <input
                type="file"
                multiple
                accept=".png,.jpg,.jpeg,.svg,.psd,image/*"
                onChange={handleImportArtwork}
                className="hidden"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          {/* Version History */}
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--studio-border)]">
            <h3 className="font-display font-semibold text-xs text-[var(--studio-text)] flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <History className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#8B7CFF]" /> Version History & Recovery
            </h3>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={versionLabel}
                onChange={(e) => setVersionLabel(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Milestone label (e.g. Clean Tie-Down Pass)..."
                className="flex-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] border border-[var(--studio-border)]"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={async () => {
                  await onSaveNamedVersion(versionLabel.trim() || 'Manual Checkpoint');
                  setVersionLabel('');
                  const list = await listProjectVersions();
                  setVersions(list);
                }}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white font-medium"
              >
                Save Snapshot
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1 max-h-32 overflow-y-auto">
              {versions.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                  No manual checkpoints yet. Continuous IndexedDB autosave is active.
                </p>
              ) : (
                versions.map((v) => (
                  <div
                    key={v.id}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)]"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-medium">{v.label}</div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[var(--studio-text-muted)]">
                        {new Date(v.savedAt).toLocaleString()}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        onUpdateProject(v.snapshot);
                        onClose();
                      }}
                      className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[#64D6C5] text-[#111827] font-semibold text-[11px]"
                    >
                      Restore
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface ExportModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  scene: Scene;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const ExportModal: React.FC<ExportModalProps> = ({ open, scene, onClose }) => {
  const [format, setFormat] = useState<'gif' | 'png-seq' | 'jpg-seq' | 'video' | 'wav'>('gif');
  const [scale, setScale] = useState(1);
  const [fps, setFps] = useState<number>(scene.fps);
  const [quality, setQuality] = useState(0.92);
  const [transparent, setTransparent] = useState(scene.transparentBg);
  const [startFrame, setStartFrame] = useState(1);
  const [endFrame, setEndFrame] = useState(scene.totalFrames);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setFps(scene.fps);
      setTransparent(scene.transparentBg);
      setStartFrame(1);
      setEndFrame(scene.totalFrames);
      setProgress(null);
      setErrorMsg(null);
    }
  }, [open, scene]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const triggerDownload = (blob: Blob, filename: string) => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleExport = async () => {
    setErrorMsg(null);
    const outW = Math.max(64, Math.round(scene.width * scale));
    const outH = Math.max(64, Math.round(scene.height * scale));
    const fStart = Math.max(0, startFrame - 1);
    const fEnd = Math.min(scene.totalFrames - 1, Math.max(fStart, endFrame - 1));
    const count = fEnd - fStart + 1;

    try {
      if (format === 'wav') {
        const wav = encodeTracksToWav(scene.audioTracks, count / fps);
        triggerDownload(new Blob([new Uint8Array(wav)], { type: 'audio/wav' }), `${scene.name}-audio.wav`);
        setProgress(100);
        return;
      }

      const off = document.createElement('canvas');
      off.width = outW;
      off.height = outH;
      const octx = off.getContext('2d')!;

      const renderToSize = (frameIdx: number) => {
        const full = document.createElement('canvas');
        full.width = scene.width;
        full.height = scene.height;
        const fctx = full.getContext('2d')!;
        renderSceneToCanvas(fctx, { ...scene, transparentBg: transparent }, {
          frame: frameIdx,
          applyCamera: true,
          applyEffects: true,
          showGuides: false,
          showRig: false,
          skipBackground: transparent,
        });
        octx.clearRect(0, 0, outW, outH);
        octx.drawImage(full, 0, 0, outW, outH);
      };

      if (format === 'gif') {
        const gifFrames = [];
        const delayCs = Math.max(2, Math.round(100 / fps));
        for (let f = fStart; f <= fEnd; f++) {
          renderToSize(f);
          const imgData = octx.getImageData(0, 0, outW, outH);
          gifFrames.push({ rgba: imgData.data, delayCs });
          setProgress(Math.round(((f - fStart + 1) / count) * 90));
        }
        const gifBytes = encodeAnimatedGif(outW, outH, gifFrames, true);
        triggerDownload(new Blob([new Uint8Array(gifBytes)], { type: 'image/gif' }), `${scene.name}.gif`);
        setProgress(100);
        return;
      }

      if (format === 'png-seq' || format === 'jpg-seq') {
        const zip = new JSZip();
        const mime = format === 'png-seq' ? 'image/png' : 'image/jpeg';
        const ext = format === 'png-seq' ? 'png' : 'jpg';
        for (let f = fStart; f <= fEnd; f++) {
          renderToSize(f);
          const blob = await new Promise<Blob>((res) =>
            off.toBlob((b) => res(b!), mime, quality)
          );
          zip.file(`frame_${String(f + 1).padStart(4, '0')}.${ext}`, blob);
          setProgress(Math.round(((f - fStart + 1) / count) * 90));
        }
        const zipBlob = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob' });
        triggerDownload(zipBlob, `${scene.name}_${ext}_sequence.zip`);
        setProgress(100);
        return;
      }

      if (format === 'video') {
        if (typeof MediaRecorder === 'undefined') {
          throw new Error('MediaRecorder video encoding is not supported in this browser.');
        }
        const mp4Type = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('video/mp4')
          ? 'video/mp4'
          : MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('video/webm;codecs=vp9')
          ? 'video/webm;codecs=vp9'
          : 'video/webm';
        const stream = off.captureStream(fps);
        const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, {
          mimeType: mp4Type,
          videoBitsPerSecond: Math.round(4_000_000 * quality),
        });
        const chunks: BlobPart[] = [];
        recorder.ondataavailable = (ev) => {
          if (ev.data.size > 0) chunks.push(ev.data);
        };
        const done = new Promise<Blob>((resolve) => {
          recorder.onstop = () => resolve(new Blob(chunks, { type: mp4Type }));
        });
        recorder.start();

        const frameMs = 1000 / fps;
        for (let f = fStart; f <= fEnd; f++) {
          renderToSize(f);
          setProgress(Math.round(((f - fStart + 1) / count) * 95));
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, frameMs));
        }
        recorder.stop();
        const videoBlob = await done;
        const ext = mp4Type.includes('mp4') ? 'mp4' : 'webm';
        triggerDownload(videoBlob, `${scene.name}.${ext}`);
        setProgress(100);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Export failed');
      setProgress(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/65 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="Export Animation"
        className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-lg bg-[var(--studio-surface)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden text-xs"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-panel)]">
          <h2 className="font-display font-semibold text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
            <Film className="w-4 h-4 text-[#8B7CFF]" /> Export Animation
          </h2>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close Export Modal" className="p-1">
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            {[
              { id: 'gif', label: 'Animated GIF' },
              { id: 'video', label: 'Video (MP4/WebM)' },
              { id: 'png-seq', label: 'PNG Seq (ZIP)' },
              { id: 'jpg-seq', label: 'JPEG Seq (ZIP)' },
              { id: 'wav', label: 'WAV Audio' },
            ].map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFormat(item.id as typeof format)}
                className={`px-2.5 py-2 rounded border font-medium text-[11px] ${
                  format === item.id
                    ? 'bg-[#8B7CFF]/15 border-[#8B7CFF] text-[#8B7CFF]'
                    : 'bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border-[var(--studio-border)]'
                }`}
              >
                {item.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] mb-1">
                Resolution Scale
              </label>
              <select
                value={scale}
                onChange={(e) => setScale(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono"
              >
                <option value={1}>100% ({scene.width}×{scene.height})</option>
                <option value={0.5}>50% ({Math.round(scene.width * 0.5)}×{Math.round(scene.height * 0.5)})</option>
                <option value={1.5}>150% ({Math.round(scene.width * 1.5)}×{Math.round(scene.height * 1.5)})</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] mb-1">
                Export Frame Rate
              </label>
              <select
                value={fps}
                onChange={(e) => setFps(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono"
              >
                <option value={12}>12 FPS</option>
                <option value={24}>24 FPS</option>
                <option value={25}>25 FPS</option>
                <option value={30}>30 FPS</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] mb-1">
                Frame Range
              </label>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={scene.totalFrames}
                  value={startFrame}
                  onChange={(e) => setStartFrame(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono"
                />
                <span>to</span>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={scene.totalFrames}
                  value={endFrame}
                  onChange={(e) => setEndFrame(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full px-2 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] font-mono"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] mb-1">
                Quality ({Math.round(quality * 100)}%)
              </label>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0.4}
                max={1}
                step={0.05}
                value={quality}
                onChange={(e) => setQuality(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full accent-[#8B7CFF]"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <label className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] cursor-pointer">
            <span>Preserve Alpha Transparency (PNG Sequence)</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={transparent}
              onChange={(e) => setTransparent(e.target.checked)}
              className="accent-[#64D6C5]"
            />
          </label>

          {errorMsg && (
            <div className="p-2 rounded bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/40 text-rose-300">
              {errorMsg}
            </div>
          )}

          {progress !== null && (
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <div className="h-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-bg)] overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className="h-full bg-[#64D6C5] transition-all"
                  style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-[#64D6C5]">
                {progress === 100 ? 'Export complete!' : `Rendering ${progress}%...`}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded border border-[var(--studio-border)]"
            >
              Close
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleExport}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded bg-[#8B7CFF] hover:bg-[#7A6AEF] text-white font-semibold"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Render & Download
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface ShortcutsModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  shortcuts: ShortcutMap;
  onClose: () => void;
  onUpdateShortcut: (action: string, key: string) => void;
}

export const ShortcutsModal: React.FC<ShortcutsModalProps> = ({
  open,
  shortcuts,
  onClose,
  onUpdateShortcut,
}) => {
  const [listeningAction, setListeningAction] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listeningAction) return;
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) parts.push('Ctrl');
      if (e.shiftKey) parts.push('Shift');
      const k = e.key === ' ' ? 'Space' : e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toUpperCase() : e.key;
      parts.push(k);
      onUpdateShortcut(listeningAction, parts.join('+'));
      setListeningAction(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler, { once: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);
  }, [listeningAction, onUpdateShortcut]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/65 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label="Keyboard Shortcuts and Workflow Guide"
        className="w-full max-w-lg max-h-[85vh] flex flex-col rounded-lg bg-[var(--studio-surface)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden text-xs"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-panel)]">
          <h2 className="font-display font-semibold text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
            <Keyboard className="w-4 h-4 text-[#64D6C5]" /> Keyboard Shortcuts & Production Guide
          </h2>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close Shortcuts" className="p-1">
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          <div className="p-3 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)] space-y-1.5">
            <div className="font-display font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#8B7CFF]">
              <FileCode2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Quick Animation Pipeline
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--studio-text-muted)] leading-relaxed">
              1. Sketch keys with Pencil (B) and toggle Onion Skin (K) to see previous/next frames.
              2. Add Keyframes or hold exposures (+1f) on the Timeline / X-Sheet.
              3. Ink with Sakuga G-Pen (N) and flood-fill with Fill Bucket (G).
              4. Add Camera keyframes, Anime Glow, or Skeletal Rig poses, then Export GIF/PNG/Video.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5">
            {Object.entries(shortcuts).map(([action, key]) => (
              <div
                key={action}
                className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded bg-[var(--studio-panel)] border border-[var(--studio-border)]"
              >
                <span className="capitalize text-[11px]">{action}</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setListeningAction(action)}
                  className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-[10px] border ${
                    listeningAction === action
                      ? 'border-[#8B7CFF] bg-[#8B7CFF] text-white'
                      : 'border-[var(--studio-border)] bg-[var(--studio-bg)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {listeningAction === action ? 'Press key...' : key}
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/* ============================================================================
   FILE: src/App.tsx
   ============================================================================ */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { PanelRightOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  decodeAudioFile,
  playTracksFromFrame,
  scrubAudioFrame,
  stopAllAudioPlayback,
} from './audio/audioEngine';
import { DrawingCanvas } from './canvas/DrawingCanvas';
import { BottomTimeline } from './components/BottomTimeline';
import { LeftToolRail } from './components/LeftToolRail';
import { ExportModal, ProjectModal, ShortcutsModal } from './components/Modals';
import { InspectorTab, RightInspector } from './components/RightInspector';
import { TopToolbar } from './components/TopToolbar';
import {
  loadAutosaveProject,
  saveAutosaveProject,
  saveVersionSnapshot,
} from './engine/projectStore';
import {
  DEFAULT_BRUSH_PRESETS,
  DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS,
  createInitialProject,
} from './engine/templates';
import {
  deleteCelById,
  duplicateCelAfter,
  insertOrUpdateCel,
  moveCelToFrame,
  resolveCelAtFrame,
  resolveLayerTransformAtFrame,
} from './engine/timeline';
import type {
  AudioTrack,
  Bone,
  BrushPreset,
  CameraKeyframe,
  CelFrame,
  CompositeEffect,
  EasingType,
  GuideConfig,
  Layer,
  MouthShapeId,
  OnionSkinConfig,
  ReferenceItem,
  Scene,
  ShortcutMap,
  StudioMode,
  StudioProject,
  ThemeMode,
  ToolId,
  TransformKeyframe,
  VectorStroke,
} from './engine/types';

export default function App() {
  const [project, setProject] = useState<StudioProject>(() => createInitialProject());
  const [historyPast, setHistoryPast] = useState<StudioProject[]>([]);
  const [historyFuture, setHistoryFuture] = useState<StudioProject[]>([]);
  const [lastSavedLabel, setLastSavedLabel] = useState('Autosaved');

  const [studioMode, setStudioMode] = useState<StudioMode>('professional');
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<ThemeMode>('dark');
  const [currentFrame, setCurrentFrame] = useState(0);
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);
  const [loopPlayback, setLoopPlayback] = useState(true);
  const [playbackSpeed, setPlaybackSpeed] = useState(1);

  const [activeLayerId, setActiveLayerId] = useState<string>('layer-character');
  const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<ToolId>('pencil');
  const [activeColor, setActiveColor] = useState('#8B7CFF');
  const [recentColors, setRecentColors] = useState<string[]>([
    '#8B7CFF',
    '#64D6C5',
    '#F3F4F6',
    '#FF6B6B',
  ]);
  const [brushPreset, setBrushPreset] = useState<BrushPreset>(DEFAULT_BRUSH_PRESETS[0]);
  const [shapeFill, setShapeFill] = useState(false);

  const selectColor = useCallback((hex: string) => {
    setActiveColor(hex);
    setRecentColors((prev) => {
      const next = [hex, ...prev.filter((c) => c.toLowerCase() !== hex.toLowerCase())];
      return next.slice(0, 8);
    });
  }, []);

  const [onionSkin, setOnionSkin] = useState<OnionSkinConfig>({
    enabled: true,
    prevFrames: 1,
    nextFrames: 1,
    opacity: 0.35,
    prevColor: '#8B7CFF',
    nextColor: '#64D6C5',
  });

  const [guides, setGuides] = useState<GuideConfig>({
    grid: false,
    gridSize: 64,
    perspective: false,
    safeArea: false,
    symmetry: 'none',
    radialSegments: 6,
  });

  const [cameraPreviewMode, setCameraPreviewMode] = useState(false);
  const [showRigOverlay, setShowRigOverlay] = useState(false);
  const [rigEditMode, setRigEditMode] = useState(false);
  const [selectedBoneId, setSelectedBoneId] = useState<string | null>('bone-hips');
  const [palmRejection, setPalmRejection] = useState(true);

  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [rotationDeg, setRotationDeg] = useState(0);

  const [inspectorOpen, setInspectorOpen] = useState(() =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth >= 1024 : true
  );
  const [inspectorTab, setInspectorTab] = useState<InspectorTab>('layers');
  const [projectModalOpen, setProjectModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [exportModalOpen, setExportModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [shortcutsModalOpen, setShortcutsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [shortcuts, setShortcuts] = useState<ShortcutMap>(DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS);

  const activeScene: Scene = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      project.scenes.find((s) => s.id === project.activeSceneId) ?? project.scenes[0]
    );
  }, [project]);

  const activeLayer: Layer | undefined = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      activeScene.layers.find((l) => l.id === activeLayerId) ?? activeScene.layers[0]
    );
  }, [activeScene.layers, activeLayerId]);

  // Ensure activeLayerId always points to an existing layer when switching scenes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeScene.layers.some((l) => l.id === activeLayerId) && activeScene.layers[0]) {
      setActiveLayerId(activeScene.layers[0].id);
    }
  }, [activeScene.layers, activeLayerId]);

  // Sync dark/light class on <html>
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (theme === 'dark') {
      root.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      root.classList.remove('dark');
    }
  }, [theme]);

  // Load IndexedDB autosave on boot (unless ?fresh=1)
  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    if (params.get('fresh') === '1') return;
    loadAutosaveProject().then((res) => {
      if (res?.project) {
        const existingIds = new Set(res.project.customBrushes.map((b) => b.id));
        const mergedBrushes = [
          ...DEFAULT_BRUSH_PRESETS.filter((b) => !existingIds.has(b.id)),
          ...res.project.customBrushes,
        ];
        setProject({ ...res.project, customBrushes: mergedBrushes });
        setLastSavedLabel('Recovered');
      }
    });
  }, []);

  // Debounced autosave
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      saveAutosaveProject(project).then(() => {
        setLastSavedLabel('Autosaved');
      });
    }, 600);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [project]);

  const projectRef = useRef(project);
  projectRef.current = project;
  const historyPastRef = useRef(historyPast);
  historyPastRef.current = historyPast;
  const historyFutureRef = useRef(historyFuture);
  historyFutureRef.current = historyFuture;

  const commitProject = useCallback(
    (updater: (prev: StudioProject) => StudioProject) => {
      const current = projectRef.current;
      const next = { ...updater(current), updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
      projectRef.current = next;
      setProject(next);
      setHistoryPast((past) => [...past.slice(-55), current]);
      setHistoryFuture([]);
    },
    []
  );

  const updateActiveScene = useCallback(
    (updater: (scene: Scene) => Scene) => {
      commitProject((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        scenes: prev.scenes.map((s) =>
          s.id === prev.activeSceneId ? updater(s) : s
        ),
      }));
    },
    [commitProject]
  );

  const handleUndo = useCallback(() => {
    const past = historyPastRef.current;
    if (past.length === 0) return;
    const previous = past[past.length - 1];
    const current = projectRef.current;
    projectRef.current = previous;
    setProject(previous);
    setHistoryPast(past.slice(0, -1));
    setHistoryFuture([current, ...historyFutureRef.current]);
  }, []);

  const handleRedo = useCallback(() => {
    const fut = historyFutureRef.current;
    if (fut.length === 0) return;
    const next = fut[0];
    const current = projectRef.current;
    projectRef.current = next;
    setProject(next);
    setHistoryPast([...historyPastRef.current, current]);
    setHistoryFuture(fut.slice(1));
  }, []);

  // Animation Playback Loop
  const playbackRef = useRef({
    frame: currentFrame,
    totalFrames: activeScene.totalFrames,
    fps: activeScene.fps,
    speed: playbackSpeed,
    loop: loopPlayback,
  });
  playbackRef.current = {
    frame: currentFrame,
    totalFrames: activeScene.totalFrames,
    fps: activeScene.fps,
    speed: playbackSpeed,
    loop: loopPlayback,
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) {
      stopAllAudioPlayback();
      return;
    }

    playTracksFromFrame(activeScene.audioTracks, currentFrame, activeScene.fps);

    let rafId = 0;
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let acc = 0;

    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const dt = now - lastTime;
      lastTime = now;
      acc += dt;
      const frameMs = 1000 / (playbackRef.current.fps * playbackRef.current.speed);

      if (acc >= frameMs) {
        acc %= frameMs;
        const nextFrame = playbackRef.current.frame + 1;
        if (nextFrame >= playbackRef.current.totalFrames) {
          if (playbackRef.current.loop) {
            setCurrentFrame(0);
            playTracksFromFrame(activeScene.audioTracks, 0, playbackRef.current.fps);
          } else {
            setIsPlaying(false);
            return;
          }
        } else {
          setCurrentFrame(nextFrame);
        }
      }
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      stopAllAudioPlayback();
    };
  }, [isPlaying, activeScene.audioTracks, activeScene.fps]);

  const handleSeekFrame = useCallback(
    (frame: number) => {
      const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(activeScene.totalFrames - 1, frame));
      setCurrentFrame(clamped);
      if (!isPlaying && activeScene.audioTracks.length > 0) {
        scrubAudioFrame(activeScene.audioTracks, clamped, activeScene.fps);
      }
    },
    [activeScene.totalFrames, activeScene.audioTracks, activeScene.fps, isPlaying]
  );

  // Keyboard shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (
        target &&
        (target.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
          target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
          target.tagName === 'SELECT')
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'z') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (e.shiftKey) handleRedo();
        else handleUndo();
        return;
      }

      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 's') {
        e.preventDefault();
        saveVersionSnapshot(project, 'Quick Save').then(() => setLastSavedLabel('Saved'));
        return;
      }

      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setIsPlaying((p) => !p);
        return;
      }

      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleSeekFrame(currentFrame - 1);
        return;
      }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        e.preventDefault();
